Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Simplify Copilot Implementation (YAGNI)

## Context

The copilot package exports functions and types that are only used internally. This violates YAGNI and exposes unnecessary API surface. This plan unexports internal-only symbols and removes a redundant method.

## Changes

### 1. Unexport internal-only functions in `transcript.go`

These 5 functions are only called from within the copilot package (by `lifecycle.go`). No external package references them.

| Current (exported) | New (unexported) |
|---|---|
| `CountLines()` | `countLines()` |
| `ExtractModifiedFilesFromLines()` | `extractModifiedFilesFromLines()` |
| `ExtractPromptsFromData()` | `extractPromptsFromData()` |
| `ExtractLastAssistantMessage()` | `extractLastAssistantMessage()` |
| `SliceFromLine()` | `sliceFromLine()` |

**Keep exported**: `ParseTranscript()` — used by `summarize/summarize.go`

### 2. Unexport internal-only types in `types.go` and `hooks.go`

| Current (exported) | New (unexported) |
|---|---|
| `HookConfig` | `hookConfig` |
| `HookEntry` | `hookEntry` |
| `FileModificationTools` | `fileModificationTools` |

**Keep exported**: `Event` — returned by `ParseTranscript()`, used by `summarize/summarize.go`

### 3. Remove `GetHookNames()` indirection

`HookNames()` in lifecycle.go delegates to `GetHookNames()` in hooks.go. Inline the list directly into `HookNames()` and remove `GetHookNames()`. Update `InstallHooks` (hooks.go:126) to call `c.HookNames()`.

### 4. Update all internal callers

In `lifecycle.go`, update calls: `CountLines` → `countLines`, etc.

### 5. Update tests

- `transcript_test.go`: Use unexported function names
- `hooks_test.go`: `TestGetHookNames` → `TestHookNames`, `HookConfig` → `hookConfig`, `HookEntry` → `hookEntry`

## Files

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `copilot/transcript.go` | Lowercase 5 function names |
| `copilot/types.go` | Lowercase `HookConfig`, `HookEntry`, `FileModificationTools` |
| `copilot/hooks.go` | Remove `GetHookNames()`, lowercase type refs |
| `copilot/lifecycle.go` | Inline `HookNames()`, lowercase function calls |
| `copilot/transcript_test.go` | Update to unexported names |
| `copilot/hooks_test.go` | Update to unexported names |
| `copilot/copilot_test.go` | Remove `TestGetSupportedHooks` if it tests removed method (verify) |

## Verification

```bash
mise run fmt && mise run lint && go test ./cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/... -count=1
```

```
     1→package copilot
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"slices"
     7→	"strings"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Copilot events.jsonl transcript parsing.
    11→//
    12→// Copilot CLI stores session transcripts as JSONL files at:
    13→//   ~/.copilot/session-state/<sessionID>/events.jsonl
    14→//
    15→// Each line is a JSON object with the schema:
    16→//   {"type":"<event-type>", "data":{...}, "id":"uuid", "timestamp":"iso8601", "parentId":"uuid|null"}
    17→//
    18→// Known event types:
    19→//   session.start, session.end, session.mode_changed, session.model_change
    20→//   user.message, assistant.message, assistant.turn_start, assistant.turn_end
    21→//   tool.execution_start, tool.execution_complete
    22→
    23→// CountLines counts the number of non-empty lines in JSONL content.
    24→func CountLines(data []byte) int {
    25→	if len(data) == 0 {
    26→		return 0
    27→	}
    28→
    29→	count := 0
    30→	lines := bytes.Split(data, []byte("\n"))
    31→	for _, line := range lines {
    32→		if len(bytes.TrimSpace(line)) > 0 {
    33→			count++
    34→		}
    35→	}
    36→	return count
    37→}
    38→
    39→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromLines extracts file paths modified by tool calls
    40→// from events.jsonl lines starting at the given offset.
    41→// Looks for tool.execution_start events where the tool name is a file modification tool.
    42→func ExtractModifiedFilesFromLines(data []byte, startOffset int) []string {
    43→	lines := bytes.Split(data, []byte("\n"))
    44→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    45→	var files []string
    46→
    47→	lineIndex := 0
    48→	for _, line := range lines {
    49→		trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line)
    50→		if len(trimmed) == 0 {
    51→			continue
    52→		}
    53→
    54→		if lineIndex < startOffset {
    55→			lineIndex++
    56→			continue
    57→		}
    58→		lineIndex++
    59→
    60→		var event Event
    61→		if err := json.Unmarshal(trimmed, &event); err != nil {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→
    65→		// Look for tool execution events that modify files
    66→		if event.Type != EventToolExecStart {
    67→			continue
    68→		}
    69→
    70→		var toolData toolExecStartData
    71→		if err := json.Unmarshal(event.Data, &toolData); err != nil {
    72→			continue
    73→		}
    74→
    75→		if !isFileModificationTool(toolData.ToolName) {
    76→			continue
    77→		}
    78→
    79→		file := extractFilePathFromArgs(toolData.Arguments)
    80→		if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
    81→			fileSet[file] = true
    82→			files = append(files, file)
    83→		}
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	return files
    87→}
    88→
    89→// ExtractPromptsFromData extracts user prompts from events.jsonl data
    90→// starting at the given line offset.
    91→// Looks for user.message events and extracts the raw content (not the transformed version
    92→// which includes injected context like timestamps and reminders).
    93→func ExtractPromptsFromData(data []byte, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    94→	lines := bytes.Split(data, []byte("\n"))
    95→	var prompts []string
    96→
    97→	lineIndex := 0
    98→	for _, line := range lines {
    99→		trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line)
   100→		if len(trimmed) == 0 {
   101→			continue
   102→		}
   103→
   104→		if lineIndex < fromOffset {
   105→			lineIndex++
   106→			continue
   107→		}
   108→		lineIndex++
   109→
   110→		var event Event
   111→		if err := json.Unmarshal(trimmed, &event); err != nil {
   112→			continue
   113→		}
   114→
   115→		if event.Type != EventUserMessage {
   116→			continue
   117→		}
   118→
   119→		var msgData userMessageData
   120→		if err := json.Unmarshal(event.Data, &msgData); err != nil {
   121→			continue
   122→		}
   123→
   124→		if msgData.Content != "" {
   125→			prompts = append(prompts, msgData.Content)
   126→		}
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	return prompts, nil
   130→}
   131→
   132→// ExtractLastAssistantMessage extracts the last non-empty assistant message
   133→// from events.jsonl data. Searches backwards for the last assistant.message
   134→// event with content.
   135→func ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data []byte) string {
   136→	lines := bytes.Split(data, []byte("\n"))
   137→
   138→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   139→		trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(lines[i])
   140→		if len(trimmed) == 0 {
   141→			continue
   142→		}
   143→
   144→		var event Event
   145→		if err := json.Unmarshal(trimmed, &event); err != nil {
   146→			continue
   147→		}
   148→
   149→		if event.Type != EventAssistantMessage {
   150→			continue
   151→		}
   152→
   153→		var msgData assistantMessageData
   154→		if err := json.Unmarshal(event.Data, &msgData); err != nil {
   155→			continue
   156→		}
   157→
   158→		if msgData.Content != "" {
   159→			return msgData.Content
   160→		}
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	return ""
   164→}
   165→
   166→// ParseTranscript parses a Copilot events.jsonl transcript into structured events.
   167→// This is used by the summarizer to build a condensed view.
   168→func ParseTranscript(data []byte) ([]Event, error) { //nolint:unparam // error kept for API consistency with other transcript parsers
   169→	lines := bytes.Split(data, []byte("\n"))
   170→	var events []Event
   171→
   172→	for _, line := range lines {
   173→		trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line)
   174→		if len(trimmed) == 0 {
   175→			continue
   176→		}
   177→
   178→		var event Event
   179→		if err := json.Unmarshal(trimmed, &event); err != nil {
   180→			continue
   181→		}
   182→		events = append(events, event)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	return events, nil
   186→}
   187→
   188→// isFileModificationTool checks if a tool name modifies files.
   189→func isFileModificationTool(toolName string) bool {
   190→	return slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, toolName)
   191→}
   192→
   193→// extractFilePathFromArgs extracts a file path from tool arguments map.
   194→// Checks common field names used by Copilot tools: path, file_path, filePath.
   195→func extractFilePathFromArgs(args map[string]any) string {
   196→	for _, key := range []string{"path", "file_path", "filePath"} {
   197→		if v, ok := args[key]; ok {
   198→			if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   199→				return s
   200→			}
   201→		}
   202→	}
   203→	return ""
   204→}
   205→
   206→// SliceFromLine returns JSONL data scoped to lines starting from startLine.
   207→// Returns the original data if startLine <= 0.
   208→// Returns nil if startLine exceeds the number of lines.
   209→func SliceFromLine(data []byte, startLine int) []byte {
   210→	if len(data) == 0 || startLine <= 0 {
   211→		return data
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	lines := bytes.Split(data, []byte("\n"))
   215→	lineIndex := 0
   216→	var result [][]byte
   217→
   218→	for _, line := range lines {
   219→		trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line)
   220→		if len(trimmed) == 0 {
   221→			continue
   222→		}
   223→
   224→		if lineIndex >= startLine {
   225→			result = append(result, line)
   226→		}
   227→		lineIndex++
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	if len(result) == 0 {
   231→		return nil
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	return []byte(strings.Join(byteSlicesToStrings(result), "\n"))
   235→}
   236→
   237→// byteSlicesToStrings converts [][]byte to []string.
   238→func byteSlicesToStrings(slices [][]byte) []string {
   239→	result := make([]string, len(slices))
   240→	for i, s := range slices {
   241→		result[i] = string(s)
   242→	}
   243→	return result
   244→}
   245→
```

```
     1→package copilot
     2→
     3→import "encoding/json"
     4→
     5→// HookConfig represents the .github/hooks/copilot-setup.json structure
     6→type HookConfig struct {
     7→	Version int                    `json:"version"`
     8→	Hooks   map[string][]HookEntry `json:"hooks"`
     9→}
    10→
    11→// HookEntry represents a single hook command entry
    12→type HookEntry struct {
    13→	Type       string `json:"type"`
    14→	Bash       string `json:"bash"`
    15→	TimeoutSec int    `json:"timeoutSec,omitempty"`
    16→}
    17→
    18→// --- Hook stdin types (from actual Copilot CLI v0.0.411 capture) ---
    19→//
    20→// Common fields: only "timestamp" (unix milliseconds) and "cwd" are present in all hooks.
    21→// "sessionId" and "transcriptPath" are only present in the agentStop hook.
    22→
    23→// hookBase contains the only fields present in ALL Copilot hook stdin JSON.
    24→type hookBase struct {
    25→	Timestamp int64  `json:"timestamp"`
    26→	Cwd       string `json:"cwd"`
    27→}
    28→
    29→// sessionStartRaw is the stdin JSON for the sessionStart hook.
    30→// Example: {"timestamp":1771465283476,"cwd":"/path","source":"new","initialPrompt":"..."}
    31→type sessionStartRaw struct {
    32→	hookBase
    33→
    34→	Source        string `json:"source,omitempty"`
    35→	InitialPrompt string `json:"initialPrompt,omitempty"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// sessionEndRaw is the stdin JSON for the sessionEnd hook.
    39→// Example: {"timestamp":1771465290000,"cwd":"/path","reason":"complete"}
    40→type sessionEndRaw struct {
    41→	hookBase
    42→
    43→	Reason string `json:"reason,omitempty"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→// userPromptRaw is the stdin JSON for the userPromptSubmitted hook.
    47→// Example: {"timestamp":1771465282293,"cwd":"/path","prompt":"..."}
    48→type userPromptRaw struct {
    49→	hookBase
    50→
    51→	Prompt string `json:"prompt,omitempty"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→// agentStopRaw is the stdin JSON for the agentStop hook.
    55→// This is the only hook that includes sessionId and transcriptPath.
    56→// Example: {"timestamp":...,"cwd":"/path","sessionId":"uuid","transcriptPath":"/path/events.jsonl","stopReason":"end_turn"}
    57→type agentStopRaw struct {
    58→	hookBase
    59→
    60→	SessionID      string `json:"sessionId"`
    61→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcriptPath"`
    62→	StopReason     string `json:"stopReason,omitempty"`
    63→}
    64→
    65→// subagentStopRaw is the stdin JSON for the subagentStop hook.
    66→// Assumed to follow agentStop format (not yet captured in the wild).
    67→type subagentStopRaw struct {
    68→	hookBase
    69→
    70→	SessionID      string `json:"sessionId,omitempty"`
    71→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcriptPath,omitempty"`
    72→}
    73→
    74→// toolUseRaw is the stdin JSON for preToolUse/postToolUse hooks.
    75→// Note: toolArgs is a JSON string in preToolUse but an object in postToolUse.
    76→// Example (pre):  {"timestamp":...,"cwd":"/path","toolName":"view","toolArgs":"{\"path\":\"/foo\"}"}
    77→// Example (post): {"timestamp":...,"cwd":"/path","toolName":"view","toolArgs":{"path":"/foo"},"toolResult":{...}}
    78→type toolUseRaw struct {
    79→	hookBase
    80→
    81→	ToolName   string          `json:"toolName"`
    82→	ToolArgs   json.RawMessage `json:"toolArgs,omitempty"`
    83→	ToolResult json.RawMessage `json:"toolResult,omitempty"`
    84→}
    85→
    86→// --- events.jsonl types (session transcript format) ---
    87→
    88→// Event represents a single line in a Copilot events.jsonl transcript.
    89→type Event struct {
    90→	Type      string          `json:"type"`
    91→	Data      json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
    92→	ID        string          `json:"id"`
    93→	Timestamp string          `json:"timestamp"`
    94→	ParentID  *string         `json:"parentId"`
    95→}
    96→
    97→// Event type constants for events.jsonl
    98→//
    99→// Known types not listed here (unused but documented):
   100→//
   101→//	session.end, session.mode_changed, session.model_change,
   102→//	assistant.turn_start, assistant.turn_end, tool.execution_complete
   103→const (
   104→	EventUserMessage      = "user.message"
   105→	EventAssistantMessage = "assistant.message"
   106→	EventToolExecStart    = "tool.execution_start"
   107→)
   108→
   109→// userMessageData is the data field for user.message events.
   110→type userMessageData struct {
   111→	Content            string `json:"content"`
   112→	TransformedContent string `json:"transformedContent"`
   113→}
   114→
   115→// assistantMessageData is the data field for assistant.message events.
   116→type assistantMessageData struct {
   117→	MessageID    string        `json:"messageId"`
   118→	Content      string        `json:"content"`
   119→	ToolRequests []toolRequest `json:"toolRequests"`
   120→}
   121→
   122→// toolRequest represents a tool call request within an assistant.message event.
   123→type toolRequest struct {
   124→	ToolCallID string         `json:"toolCallId"`
   125→	Name       string         `json:"name"`
   126→	Arguments  map[string]any `json:"arguments"`
   127→	Type       string         `json:"type"`
   128→}
   129→
   130→// toolExecStartData is the data field for tool.execution_start events.
   131→type toolExecStartData struct {
   132→	ToolCallID string         `json:"toolCallId"`
   133→	ToolName   string         `json:"toolName"`
   134→	Arguments  map[string]any `json:"arguments"`
   135→}
   136→
   137→// Tool names used in GitHub Copilot that modify files
   138→const (
   139→	ToolEdit   = "edit"
   140→	ToolCreate = "create"
   141→)
   142→
   143→// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files in GitHub Copilot
   144→var FileModificationTools = []string{
   145→	ToolEdit,
   146→	ToolCreate,
   147→}
   148→
```

```
     1→package copilot
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Ensure CopilotAgent implements HookSupport and HookHandler
    15→var (
    16→	_ agent.HookSupport = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    17→	_ agent.HookHandler = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    18→)
    19→
    20→// Copilot hook names - these become subcommands under `entire hooks github-copilot`
    21→const (
    22→	HookNameSessionStart        = "session-start"
    23→	HookNameSessionEnd          = "session-end"
    24→	HookNameUserPromptSubmitted = "user-prompt-submitted"
    25→	HookNameAgentStop           = "agent-stop"
    26→	HookNameSubagentStop        = "subagent-stop"
    27→	HookNamePreToolUse          = "pre-tool-use"
    28→	HookNamePostToolUse         = "post-tool-use"
    29→	HookNameErrorOccurred       = "error-occurred"
    30→)
    31→
    32→// CopilotConfigFileName is the hook config file used by GitHub Copilot.
    33→const CopilotConfigFileName = "copilot-setup.json"
    34→
    35→// hookNameToConfigKey maps CLI subcommand names to Copilot's native hook names (camelCase in config)
    36→var hookNameToConfigKey = map[string]string{
    37→	HookNameSessionStart:        "sessionStart",
    38→	HookNameSessionEnd:          "sessionEnd",
    39→	HookNameUserPromptSubmitted: "userPromptSubmitted",
    40→	HookNameAgentStop:           "agentStop",
    41→	HookNameSubagentStop:        "subagentStop",
    42→	HookNamePreToolUse:          "preToolUse",
    43→	HookNamePostToolUse:         "postToolUse",
    44→	HookNameErrorOccurred:       "errorOccurred",
    45→}
    46→
    47→// entireHookPrefixes are command prefixes that identify Entire hooks
    48→var entireHookPrefixes = []string{
    49→	"entire ",
    50→	"go run ${COPILOT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go ",
    51→}
    52→
    53→// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs Copilot supports.
    54→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
    55→	return []string{
    56→		HookNameSessionStart,
    57→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    58→		HookNameUserPromptSubmitted,
    59→		HookNameAgentStop,
    60→		HookNameSubagentStop,
    61→		HookNamePreToolUse,
    62→		HookNamePostToolUse,
    63→		HookNameErrorOccurred,
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// InstallHooks installs Copilot hooks in .github/hooks/copilot-setup.json.
    68→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    69→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    70→func (c *CopilotAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    71→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    72→	if err != nil {
    73→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    74→		if err != nil {
    75→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    76→		}
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
    80→
    81→	// Read existing config if it exists
    82→	var config HookConfig
    83→	existingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
    84→	if readErr == nil {
    85→		if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &config); err != nil {
    86→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse existing %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
    87→		}
    88→	} else {
    89→		config.Version = 1
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	if config.Hooks == nil {
    93→		config.Hooks = make(map[string][]HookEntry)
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Define hook command based on localDev mode
    97→	var cmdPrefix string
    98→	if localDev {
    99→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${COPILOT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks github-copilot "
   100→	} else {
   101→		cmdPrefix = "entire hooks github-copilot "
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	// Check for idempotency BEFORE removing hooks
   105→	if !force {
   106→		configKey := hookNameToConfigKey[HookNameSessionStart]
   107→		expectedCmd := cmdPrefix + HookNameSessionStart
   108→		if entries, ok := config.Hooks[configKey]; ok {
   109→			for _, entry := range entries {
   110→				if entry.Bash == expectedCmd {
   111→					return 0, nil // Already installed with same mode
   112→				}
   113→			}
   114→		}
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	// Remove existing Entire hooks first
   118→	for configKey, entries := range config.Hooks {
   119→		config.Hooks[configKey] = removeEntireHookEntries(entries)
   120→		if len(config.Hooks[configKey]) == 0 {
   121→			delete(config.Hooks, configKey)
   122→		}
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	// Install all 8 hooks
   126→	hookNames := c.GetHookNames()
   127→	for _, hookName := range hookNames {
   128→		configKey, ok := hookNameToConfigKey[hookName]
   129→		if !ok {
   130→			continue
   131→		}
   132→
   133→		entry := HookEntry{
   134→			Type:       "command",
   135→			Bash:       cmdPrefix + hookName,
   136→			TimeoutSec: 10,
   137→		}
   138→
   139→		config.Hooks[configKey] = append(config.Hooks[configKey], entry)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	count := len(hookNames)
   143→
   144→	// Write config file
   145→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(configPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   146→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .github/hooks directory: %w", err)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(config, "", "  ")
   150→	if err != nil {
   151→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal config: %w", err)
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	if err := os.WriteFile(configPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   155→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	return count, nil
   159→}
   160→
   161→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Copilot config.
   162→func (c *CopilotAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   163→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		repoRoot = "."
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
   169→	data, err := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   170→	if err != nil {
   171→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No config file means nothing to uninstall
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	var config HookConfig
   175→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   176→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	if config.Hooks == nil {
   180→		return nil
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   184→	for configKey, entries := range config.Hooks {
   185→		config.Hooks[configKey] = removeEntireHookEntries(entries)
   186→		if len(config.Hooks[configKey]) == 0 {
   187→			delete(config.Hooks, configKey)
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Write back
   192→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(config, "", "  ")
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal config: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	if err := os.WriteFile(configPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	return nil
   202→}
   203→
   204→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed in Copilot config.
   205→func (c *CopilotAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   206→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   207→	if err != nil {
   208→		repoRoot = "."
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
   212→	data, err := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		return false
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	var config HookConfig
   218→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   219→		return false
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	for _, entries := range config.Hooks {
   223→		for _, entry := range entries {
   224→			if isEntireHook(entry.Bash) {
   225→				return true
   226→			}
   227→		}
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	return false
   231→}
   232→
   233→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Copilot supports.
   234→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   235→	return []agent.HookType{
   236→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   237→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   238→		agent.HookStop,
   239→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   240→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   241→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   242→	}
   243→}
   244→
   245→// Helper functions
   246→
   247→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook
   248→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   249→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   250→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   251→			return true
   252→		}
   253→	}
   254→	return false
   255→}
   256→
   257→// removeEntireHookEntries removes all Entire hook entries from a slice
   258→func removeEntireHookEntries(entries []HookEntry) []HookEntry {
   259→	result := make([]HookEntry, 0, len(entries))
   260→	for _, entry := range entries {
   261→		if !isEntireHook(entry.Bash) {
   262→			result = append(result, entry)
   263→		}
   264→	}
   265→	return result
   266→}
   267→
```

```
     1→package copilot

     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"

     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Compile-time interface assertions
    13→var _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    14→
    15→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Copilot supports.
    16→func (c *CopilotAgent) HookNames() []string {
    17→	return c.GetHookNames()
    18→}
    19→
    20→// ParseHookEvent translates a Copilot hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    21→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    22→//
    23→// Note: In Copilot CLI, only agentStop provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
    24→// Other hooks (sessionStart, userPromptSubmitted, sessionEnd) do not include
    25→// these fields, so the resulting Event will have empty SessionID/SessionRef.
    26→func (c *CopilotAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    27→	switch hookName {
    28→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    29→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    30→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmitted:
    31→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    32→	case HookNameAgentStop:
    33→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    34→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    35→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    36→	case HookNameSubagentStop:
    37→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    38→	case HookNamePreToolUse, HookNamePostToolUse, HookNameErrorOccurred:
    39→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    40→	default:
    41→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    42→	}
    43→}
    44→
    45→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    46→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    47→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    50→	}
    51→	return data, nil
    52→}
    53→
    54→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript into chunks.
    55→func (c *CopilotAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    56→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→	return chunks, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// ReassembleTranscript combines JSONL chunks back into a single transcript.
    64→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
    65→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
    66→}
    67→
    68→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    69→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    70→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	return ExtractPromptsFromData(data, fromOffset)
    76→}
    77→
    78→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    79→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    80→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    83→	}
    84→	return ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data), nil
    85→}
    86→
    87→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.
    88→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    89→	if path == "" {
    90→		return 0, nil
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    96→			return 0, nil
    97→		}
    98→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	return CountLines(data), nil
   102→}
   103→
   104→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.
   105→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   106→	if path == "" {
   107→		return nil, 0, nil
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   111→	if readErr != nil {
   112→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   113→			return nil, 0, nil
   114→		}
   115→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	if len(data) == 0 {
   119→		return nil, 0, nil
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	totalLines := CountLines(data)
   123→	extractedFiles := ExtractModifiedFilesFromLines(data, startOffset)
   124→
   125→	return extractedFiles, totalLines, nil
   126→}
   127→
   128→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   129→
   130→// parseSessionStart handles the sessionStart hook.
   131→// Note: Copilot's sessionStart stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   132→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   133→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionStartRaw](stdin)
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return nil, err
   136→	}
   137→	return &agent.Event{
   138→		Type:      agent.SessionStart,
   139→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   140→	}, nil
   141→}
   142→
   143→// parseTurnStart handles the userPromptSubmitted hook.
   144→// Note: Copilot's userPromptSubmitted stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   145→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   146→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptRaw](stdin)
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		return nil, err
   149→	}
   150→	return &agent.Event{
   151→		Type:      agent.TurnStart,
   152→		Prompt:    raw.Prompt,
   153→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   154→	}, nil
   155→}
   156→
   157→// parseTurnEnd handles the agentStop hook.
   158→// This is the only hook that provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
   159→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   160→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[agentStopRaw](stdin)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return nil, err
   163→	}
   164→	return &agent.Event{
   165→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   166→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   167→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   168→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   169→	}, nil
   170→}
   171→
   172→// parseSessionEnd handles the sessionEnd hook.
   173→// Note: Copilot's sessionEnd stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   174→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   175→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionEndRaw](stdin)
   176→	if err != nil {
   177→		return nil, err
   178→	}
   179→	return &agent.Event{
   180→		Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   181→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   182→	}, nil
   183→}
   184→
   185→// parseSubagentEnd handles the subagentStop hook.
   186→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   187→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[subagentStopRaw](stdin)
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		return nil, err
   190→	}
   191→	return &agent.Event{
   192→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   193→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   194→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   195→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   196→	}, nil
   197→}
```

```
    35→func TestCountLines(t *testing.T) {
    36→	t.Parallel()
    37→
    38→	tests := []struct {
    39→		name string
    40→		data string
    41→		want int
    42→	}{
    43→		{"empty", "", 0},
    44→		{"single line", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}`, 1},
    45→		{"two lines", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}
    46→{"type":"user.message","data":{}}`, 2},
    47→		{"trailing newline", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}
    48→`, 1},
    49→		{"blank lines ignored", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}
    50→
    51→{"type":"user.message","data":{}}
    52→`, 2},
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	for _, tt := range tests {
    56→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
    57→			t.Parallel()
    58→			got := CountLines([]byte(tt.data))
    59→			if got != tt.want {
    60→				t.Errorf("CountLines() = %d, want %d", got, tt.want)
    61→			}
    62→		})
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→func TestExtractModifiedFilesFromLines(t *testing.T) {
    67→	t.Parallel()
    68→
    69→	files := ExtractModifiedFilesFromLines([]byte(sampleEventsWithCreate), 0)
    70→
    71→	if len(files) != 2 {
    72→		t.Fatalf("ExtractModifiedFilesFromLines() got %d files, want 2: %v", len(files), files)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	expectedFiles := map[string]bool{"/tmp/test.txt": true, "/tmp/other.go": true}
    76→	for _, f := range files {
    77→		if !expectedFiles[f] {
    78→			t.Errorf("unexpected file: %s", f)
    79→		}
    80→	}
    81→}
```

```
   293→// Helper functions
   294→
   295→func readCopilotConfig(t *testing.T, tempDir string) HookConfig {
   296→	t.Helper()
   297→	configPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
   298→	data, err := os.ReadFile(configPath)
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		t.Fatalf("failed to read config: %v", err)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	var config HookConfig
   304→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   305→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse config: %v", err)
   306→	}
   307→	return config
   308→}
   309→
   310→func writeCopilotConfig(t *testing.T, tempDir, content string) {
   311→	t.Helper()
   312→	hooksDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".github", "hooks")
   313→	if err := os.MkdirAll(hooksDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   314→		t.Fatalf("failed to create hooks dir: %v", err)
   315→	}
   316→	configPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, CopilotConfigFileName)
   317→	if err := os.WriteFile(configPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   318→		t.Fatalf("failed to write config: %v", err)
   319→	}
   320→}
```

```
    12→func TestNewCopilotAgent(t *testing.T) {
    13→	t.Parallel()
    14→
    15→	ag := NewCopilotAgent()
    16→	if ag == nil {
    17→		t.Fatal("NewCopilotAgent() returned nil")
    18→	}
    19→

    24→	if cp == nil {

   194→func TestParseHookInput_SessionStart(t *testing.T) {
   195→	t.Parallel()

   199→	input := `{"timestamp":1771465283476,"cwd":"/project","source":"new","initialPrompt":"hello"}`
   200→
   201→	hookInput, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, bytes.NewReader([]byte(input)))

   206→	// sessionStart does NOT provide sessionId or transcriptPath

   209→	}

   212→	}

   215→	}

   218→	}
   219→}

   491→func TestChunkTranscript_RoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
   492→	t.Parallel()
   493→
   494→	ag := &CopilotAgent{}
   495→	original := `{"role":"user","content":"hello"}
   496→{"role":"assistant","content":"hi there"}
   497→{"role":"user","content":"thanks"}`
   498→
   499→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript([]byte(original), 50)
   500→	if err != nil {
   501→		t.Fatalf("ChunkTranscript() error = %v", err)
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	reassembled, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   505→	if err != nil {
   506→		t.Fatalf("ReassembleTranscript() error = %v", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	if string(reassembled) != original {
   510→		t.Errorf("Round-trip failed:\ngot:  %q\nwant: %q", string(reassembled), original)
   511→	}
   512→}
```

```
     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:
    15→//
    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent
    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript
    19→//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase
    20→type Agent interface {
    21→	// --- Identity ---
    22→
    23→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    24→	Name() AgentName
    25→
    26→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    27→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    28→	Type() AgentType
    29→
    30→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    31→	Description() string
    32→
    33→	// IsPreview returns whether the agent integration is in preview or stable
    34→	IsPreview() bool
    35→
    36→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    37→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    38→
    39→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    40→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    41→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    42→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    43→
    44→	// --- Event Mapping ---
    45→
    46→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    47→	// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    48→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "session-start", "session-end"]
    49→	HookNames() []string
    50→
    51→	// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    52→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    53→	// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.
    54→	ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    55→
    56→	// --- Transcript Storage ---
    57→
    58→	// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.
    59→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    60→
    61→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
    62→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
    63→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
    64→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    65→
    66→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    67→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
    68→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    69→
    70→	// --- Legacy methods (will move to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---
    71→
    72→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none).
    73→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    74→
    75→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks.
```

```
     1→package copilot
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Ensure CopilotAgent implements HookSupport and HookHandler
    15→var (
    16→	_ agent.HookSupport = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    17→	_ agent.HookHandler = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    18→)
    19→
    20→// Copilot hook names - these become subcommands under `entire hooks github-copilot`
    21→const (
    22→	HookNameSessionStart        = "session-start"
    23→	HookNameSessionEnd          = "session-end"
    24→	HookNameUserPromptSubmitted = "user-prompt-submitted"
    25→	HookNameAgentStop           = "agent-stop"
    26→	HookNameSubagentStop        = "subagent-stop"
    27→	HookNamePreToolUse          = "pre-tool-use"
    28→	HookNamePostToolUse         = "post-tool-use"
    29→	HookNameErrorOccurred       = "error-occurred"
    30→)
    31→
    32→// CopilotConfigFileName is the hook config file used by GitHub Copilot.
    33→const CopilotConfigFileName = "copilot-setup.json"
    34→
    35→// hookNameToConfigKey maps CLI subcommand names to Copilot's native hook names (camelCase in config)
    36→var hookNameToConfigKey = map[string]string{
    37→	HookNameSessionStart:        "sessionStart",
    38→	HookNameSessionEnd:          "sessionEnd",
    39→	HookNameUserPromptSubmitted: "userPromptSubmitted",
    40→	HookNameAgentStop:           "agentStop",
    41→	HookNameSubagentStop:        "subagentStop",
    42→	HookNamePreToolUse:          "preToolUse",
    43→	HookNamePostToolUse:         "postToolUse",
    44→	HookNameErrorOccurred:       "errorOccurred",
    45→}
    46→
    47→// entireHookPrefixes are command prefixes that identify Entire hooks
    48→var entireHookPrefixes = []string{
    49→	"entire ",
    50→	"go run ${COPILOT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go ",
    51→}
    52→
    53→// GetHookNames implements agent.HookHandler by delegating to HookNames.
    54→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
    55→	return c.HookNames()
    56→}
    57→
    58→// InstallHooks installs Copilot hooks in .github/hooks/copilot-setup.json.
    59→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    60→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    61→func (c *CopilotAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    62→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    63→	if err != nil {
    64→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    65→		if err != nil {
    66→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    67→		}
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
    71→
    72→	// Read existing config if it exists
    73→	var config hookConfig
    74→	existingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
    75→	if readErr == nil {
    76→		if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &config); err != nil {
    77→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse existing %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
    78→		}
    79→	} else {
    80→		config.Version = 1
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	if config.Hooks == nil {
    84→		config.Hooks = make(map[string][]hookEntry)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Define hook command based on localDev mode
    88→	var cmdPrefix string
    89→	if localDev {
    90→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${COPILOT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks github-copilot "
    91→	} else {
    92→		cmdPrefix = "entire hooks github-copilot "
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Check for idempotency BEFORE removing hooks
    96→	if !force {
    97→		configKey := hookNameToConfigKey[HookNameSessionStart]
    98→		expectedCmd := cmdPrefix + HookNameSessionStart
    99→		if entries, ok := config.Hooks[configKey]; ok {
   100→			for _, entry := range entries {
   101→				if entry.Bash == expectedCmd {
   102→					return 0, nil // Already installed with same mode
   103→				}
   104→			}
   105→		}
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Remove existing Entire hooks first
   109→	for configKey, entries := range config.Hooks {
   110→		config.Hooks[configKey] = removeEntireHookEntries(entries)
   111→		if len(config.Hooks[configKey]) == 0 {
   112→			delete(config.Hooks, configKey)
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Install all 8 hooks
   117→	hookNames := c.HookNames()
   118→	for _, hookName := range hookNames {
   119→		configKey, ok := hookNameToConfigKey[hookName]
   120→		if !ok {
   121→			continue
   122→		}
   123→
   124→		entry := hookEntry{
   125→			Type:       "command",
   126→			Bash:       cmdPrefix + hookName,
   127→			TimeoutSec: 10,
   128→		}
   129→
   130→		config.Hooks[configKey] = append(config.Hooks[configKey], entry)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	count := len(hookNames)
   134→
   135→	// Write config file
   136→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(configPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   137→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .github/hooks directory: %w", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(config, "", "  ")
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal config: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	if err := os.WriteFile(configPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   146→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	return count, nil
   150→}
   151→
   152→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Copilot config.
   153→func (c *CopilotAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   154→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		repoRoot = "."
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
   160→	data, err := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No config file means nothing to uninstall
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	var config hookConfig
   166→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   167→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	if config.Hooks == nil {
   171→		return nil
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   175→	for configKey, entries := range config.Hooks {
   176→		config.Hooks[configKey] = removeEntireHookEntries(entries)
   177→		if len(config.Hooks[configKey]) == 0 {
   178→			delete(config.Hooks, configKey)
   179→		}
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	// Write back
   183→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(config, "", "  ")
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal config: %w", err)
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	if err := os.WriteFile(configPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   189→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	return nil
   193→}
   194→
   195→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed in Copilot config.
   196→func (c *CopilotAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   197→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		repoRoot = "."
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
   203→	data, err := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return false
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	var config hookConfig
   209→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   210→		return false
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	for _, entries := range config.Hooks {
   214→		for _, entry := range entries {
   215→			if isEntireHook(entry.Bash) {
   216→				return true
   217→			}
   218→		}
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	return false
   222→}
   223→
   224→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Copilot supports.
   225→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   226→	return []agent.HookType{
   227→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   228→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   229→		agent.HookStop,
   230→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   231→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   232→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   233→	}
   234→}
   235→
   236→// Helper functions
   237→
   238→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook
   239→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   240→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   241→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   242→			return true
   243→		}
   244→	}
   245→	return false
   246→}
   247→
   248→// removeEntireHookEntries removes all Entire hook entries from a slice
   249→func removeEntireHookEntries(entries []hookEntry) []hookEntry {
   250→	result := make([]hookEntry, 0, len(entries))
   251→	for _, entry := range entries {
   252→		if !isEntireHook(entry.Bash) {
   253→			result = append(result, entry)
   254→		}
   255→	}
   256→	return result
   257→}
   258→
```

```
     1→package copilot
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"io"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"time"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Compile-time interface assertions
    13→var _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    14→
    15→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Copilot supports.
    16→func (c *CopilotAgent) HookNames() []string {
    17→	return []string{
    18→		HookNameSessionStart,
    19→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    20→		HookNameUserPromptSubmitted,
    21→		HookNameAgentStop,
    22→		HookNameSubagentStop,
    23→		HookNamePreToolUse,
    24→		HookNamePostToolUse,
    25→		HookNameErrorOccurred,
    26→	}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// ParseHookEvent translates a Copilot hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    30→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    31→//
    32→// Note: In Copilot CLI, only agentStop provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
    33→// Other hooks (sessionStart, userPromptSubmitted, sessionEnd) do not include
    34→// these fields, so the resulting Event will have empty SessionID/SessionRef.
    35→func (c *CopilotAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmitted:
    40→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNameAgentStop:
    42→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    44→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    45→	case HookNameSubagentStop:
    46→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    47→	case HookNamePreToolUse, HookNamePostToolUse, HookNameErrorOccurred:
    48→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    49→	default:
    50→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    51→	}
    52→}
    53→
    54→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    55→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    56→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→	return data, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript into chunks.
    64→func (c *CopilotAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    65→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→	return chunks, nil
    70→}
    71→
    72→// ReassembleTranscript combines JSONL chunks back into a single transcript.
    73→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
    74→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
    75→}
    76→
    77→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    78→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    79→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	return extractPromptsFromData(data, fromOffset)
    85→}
    86→
    87→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    88→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    89→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    90→	if err != nil {
    91→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    92→	}
    93→	return extractLastAssistantMessage(data), nil
    94→}
    95→
    96→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.
    97→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    98→	if path == "" {
    99→		return 0, nil
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   105→			return 0, nil
   106→		}
   107→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return countLines(data), nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.
   114→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   115→	if path == "" {
   116→		return nil, 0, nil
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   120→	if readErr != nil {
   121→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   122→			return nil, 0, nil
   123→		}
   124→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	if len(data) == 0 {
   128→		return nil, 0, nil
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	totalLines := countLines(data)
   132→	extractedFiles := extractModifiedFilesFromLines(data, startOffset)
   133→
   134→	return extractedFiles, totalLines, nil
   135→}
   136→
   137→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   138→
   139→// parseSessionStart handles the sessionStart hook.
   140→// Note: Copilot's sessionStart stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   141→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   142→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionStartRaw](stdin)
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		return nil, err
   145→	}
   146→	return &agent.Event{
   147→		Type:      agent.SessionStart,
   148→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   149→	}, nil
   150→}
   151→
   152→// parseTurnStart handles the userPromptSubmitted hook.
   153→// Note: Copilot's userPromptSubmitted stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   154→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   155→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptRaw](stdin)
   156→	if err != nil {
   157→		return nil, err
   158→	}
   159→	return &agent.Event{
   160→		Type:      agent.TurnStart,
   161→		Prompt:    raw.Prompt,
   162→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   163→	}, nil
   164→}
   165→
   166→// parseTurnEnd handles the agentStop hook.
   167→// This is the only hook that provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
   168→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   169→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[agentStopRaw](stdin)
   170→	if err != nil {
   171→		return nil, err
   172→	}
   173→	return &agent.Event{
   174→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   175→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   176→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   177→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   178→	}, nil
   179→}
   180→
   181→// parseSessionEnd handles the sessionEnd hook.
   182→// Note: Copilot's sessionEnd stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   183→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   184→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionEndRaw](stdin)
   185→	if err != nil {
   186→		return nil, err
   187→	}
   188→	return &agent.Event{
   189→		Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   190→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   191→	}, nil
   192→}
   193→
   194→// parseSubagentEnd handles the subagentStop hook.
   195→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   196→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[subagentStopRaw](stdin)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return nil, err
   199→	}
   200→	return &agent.Event{
   201→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   202→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   203→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   204→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   205→	}, nil
   206→}
   207→
```

```
    35→func TestcountLines(t *testing.T) {
    36→	t.Parallel()
    37→
    38→	tests := []struct {
    39→		name string
    40→		data string
    41→		want int
    42→	}{
    43→		{"empty", "", 0},
    44→		{"single line", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}`, 1},
    45→		{"two lines", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}
    46→{"type":"user.message","data":{}}`, 2},
    47→		{"trailing newline", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}
    48→`, 1},
    49→		{"blank lines ignored", `{"type":"session.start","data":{}}
    50→
    51→{"type":"user.message","data":{}}
    52→`, 2},
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	for _, tt := range tests {
    56→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
    57→			t.Parallel()
    58→			got := countLines([]byte(tt.data))
    59→			if got != tt.want {
    60→				t.Errorf("countLines() = %d, want %d", got, tt.want)
    61→			}
    62→		})
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→func TestextractModifiedFilesFromLines(t *testing.T) {
    67→	t.Parallel()
    68→
    69→	files := extractModifiedFilesFromLines([]byte(sampleEventsWithCreate), 0)
    70→
    71→	if len(files) != 2 {
    72→		t.Fatalf("extractModifiedFilesFromLines() got %d files, want 2: %v", len(files), files)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	expectedFiles := map[string]bool{"/tmp/test.txt": true, "/tmp/other.go": true}
    76→	for _, f := range files {
    77→		if !expectedFiles[f] {
    78→			t.Errorf("unexpected file: %s", f)
    79→		}
    80→	}
    81→}

   181→	// sampleEventsWithCreate has assistant messages with empty content (tool-only) and one final message

   183→	if result != "Done! Created test.txt and edited other.go." {

   197→func TestextractLastAssistantMessage_NoAssistant(t *testing.T) {
   198→	t.Parallel()

   200→	data := `{"type":"user.message","data":{"content":"hello"},"id":"e1","timestamp":"2025-01-01T00:00:00Z","parentId":null}`
   201→	result := extractLastAssistantMessage([]byte(data))
   202→	if result != "" {

   207→func TestsliceFromLine(t *testing.T) {
   208→	t.Parallel()
   209→
   210→	data := []byte(`{"type":"session.start","data":{},"id":"e1","timestamp":"T","parentId":null}
   211→{"type":"user.message","data":{"content":"hi"},"id":"e2","timestamp":"T","parentId":"e1"}
   212→{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"content":"hello"},"id":"e3","timestamp":"T","parentId":"e2"}`)
   213→
   214→	t.Run("start from 0", func(t *testing.T) {
   215→		t.Parallel()
   216→		result := sliceFromLine(data, 0)
   217→		if string(result) != string(data) {
   218→			t.Errorf("sliceFromLine(0) should return original data")
   219→		}
   220→	})
   221→
   222→	t.Run("start from 1", func(t *testing.T) {
   223→		t.Parallel()
   224→		result := sliceFromLine(data, 1)
   225→		if result == nil {
   226→			t.Fatal("sliceFromLine(1) returned nil")
   227→		}
   228→		lines := countLines(result)
   229→		if lines != 2 {
   230→			t.Errorf("sliceFromLine(1) has %d lines, want 2", lines)
   231→		}
   232→	})
   233→
   234→	t.Run("start past end", func(t *testing.T) {
   235→		t.Parallel()
   236→		result := sliceFromLine(data, 10)
   237→		if result != nil {
   238→			t.Errorf("sliceFromLine(10) should return nil, got %q", result)
   239→		}
   240→	})
   241→
   242→	t.Run("empty data", func(t *testing.T) {
   243→		t.Parallel()
   244→		result := sliceFromLine([]byte(""), 0)
   245→		if len(result) != 0 {
   246→			t.Errorf("sliceFromLine on empty data should return empty")
   247→		}
   248→	})
   249→}

   297→func TestExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(t *testing.T) {

   333→func TestParseTranscript(t *testing.T) {

   350→		"assistant.turn_start",

   359→
   360→	for i, expected := range expectedTypes {

   363→		}
```

Perfect! I now have a comprehensive understanding of the e2e test infrastructure. Let me compile my findings into a detailed report.

## E2E Test Infrastructure - Comprehensive Exploration

I've thoroughly analyzed the e2e test infrastructure in this codebase. Here's what I discovered:

---

### 1. E2E Test Structure

**Location:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/`

**Key Files:**
- `setup_test.go` (90 lines) - TestMain initialization and binary building
- `testenv.go` (1,134 lines) - Core test environment and helpers
- `agent_runner.go` (326 lines) - AgentRunner interface and implementations
- `assertions.go` (158 lines) - Test assertion helpers
- `prompts.go` (114 lines) - Deterministic prompt templates
- `scenario_*.go` - Scenario tests (7 files, ~1,800 lines total)

**Build Tag:** All files use `//go:build e2e` - tests are excluded from normal test runs and only execute when explicitly tagged.

---

### 2. E2E_AGENT Environment Variable

**Default Agent Selection** (`setup_test.go:18-21`):
```go
defaultAgent = os.Getenv("E2E_AGENT")
if defaultAgent == "" {
    defaultAgent = AgentNameClaudeCode
}
```

**How It Works:**
- TestMain reads the `E2E_AGENT` env var at startup
- Falls back to `"claude-code"` if not set
- The agent is checked for availability before any tests run
- If unavailable, all e2e tests are skipped gracefully (exit 0, not failure)

**Supported Agents** (`agent_runner.go:55-65`):
- `"claude-code"` → ClaudeCodeRunner
- `"gemini"` → GeminiCLIRunner
- Unknown agents → unavailableRunner (reports not available)

---

### 3. Agent Instantiation and the Factory Pattern

**AgentRunner Interface** (`agent_runner.go:24-36`):
```go
type AgentRunner interface {
    Name() string
    IsAvailable() (bool, error)
    RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error)
    RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error)
}
```

**Factory Function** (`agent_runner.go:52-65`):
```go
func NewAgentRunner(name string, config AgentRunnerConfig) AgentRunner {
    switch name {
    case AgentNameClaudeCode:
        return NewClaudeCodeRunner(config)
    case AgentNameGemini:
        return NewGeminiCLIRunner(config)
    default:
        return &unavailableRunner{name: name}
    }
}
```

**Test Environment Usage** (`testenv.go:47-65`):
```go
func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    // ... setup
    agent := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
    return &TestEnv{
        T:       t,
        RepoDir: repoDir,
        Agent:   agent,
    }
}
```

Each test creates a `TestEnv` which internally creates an AgentRunner via the factory.

---

### 4. Claude Code Agent-Specific Setup

**Availability Check** (`agent_runner.go:126-146`):
- Checks if `claude` CLI is in PATH via `exec.LookPath("claude")`
- Runs `claude --version` with a 10-second timeout to verify it's working
- **Does NOT check authentication** - OAuth login is assumed via `claude login`
- If not available, tests skip gracefully

**Configuration** (`agent_runner.go:93-119`):
```go
type ClaudeCodeRunner struct {
    Model        string
    Timeout      time.Duration
    AllowedTools []string
}
```

**Environment Variables for Claude:**
- `E2E_CLAUDE_MODEL` - Model to use (default: `"haiku"` for cost efficiency)
- `E2E_TIMEOUT` - Per-prompt timeout (default: 2 minutes)

**Default Allowed Tools:**
```go
AllowedTools: []string{"Edit", "Read", "Write", "Bash", "Glob", "Grep"}
```

Tests can override tools via `RunAgentWithTools()`.

**Command Construction** (`agent_runner.go:152-164`):
```go
cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", 
    "--model", r.Model,
    "-p", prompt,
    "--allowedTools", tools...)
cmd.Dir = workDir
```

The runner **invokes the actual `claude` CLI** with the prompt and allowed tools.

---

### 5. Test Workflow

**High-level flow** (see `scenario_basic_workflow_test.go`):

1. **Setup** (`NewFeatureBranchEnv`)
   - Initialize git repo
   - Create initial commit on main
   - Checkout feature branch
   - Run `entire enable --agent <agent> --strategy <strategy>` to set up hooks and settings
   - Commit hook configuration

2. **Agent Invocation** (`RunAgent` / `RunAgentWithTools`)
   - Invoke `claude` CLI with prompt
   - CLI runs in test repo directory
   - Returns `AgentResult` with stdout, stderr, exit code, duration
   - No simulated hooks - agent interaction is real

3. **Checkpoint Creation** (`GitCommitWithShadowHooks`)
   - Stage files created by agent
   - Run `entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg` hook manually
   - Create commit using go-git with modified message
   - Run `entire hooks git post-commit` hook manually
   - Hooks trigger checkpoint creation and metadata storage

4. **Verification**
   - Check rewind points via `entire rewind --list`
   - Verify checkpoint trailers in commit messages
   - Validate metadata on `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch
   - Check for expected files and content

**Key Detail:** Tests invoke the **real agent CLI**, not mocks. The hooks are called manually in e2e tests via direct CLI invocation.

---

### 6. Agent Registry Pattern (in Main CLI)

The main CLI code (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/`) uses a central registry pattern:

**Registry in `cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go`:**
```go
var registry = make(map[AgentName]Factory)

func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) { ... }
func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) { ... }
func List() []AgentName { ... }
func Detect() (Agent, error) { ... }
```

**Agent Self-Registration** (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go:20-23`):
```go
func init() {
    agent.Register(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode, NewClaudeCodeAgent)
}
```

Each agent package has an `init()` function that registers itself automatically when imported.

**Hook Command Registration** (`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_cmd.go:13-37`):
```go
func newHooksCmd() *cobra.Command {
    cmd := &cobra.Command{ Use: "hooks", ... }
    cmd.AddCommand(newHooksGitCmd())
    
    // Dynamically add agent hook subcommands
    for _, agentName := range agent.List() {
        ag, err := agent.Get(agentName)
        if handler, ok := ag.(agent.HookHandler); ok {
            cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHooksCmd(agentName, handler))
        }
    }
    return cmd
}
```

The CLI automatically discovers registered agents and creates hook commands for each.

**Agent Auto-Detection:**
```go
func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    // Returns first agent whose DetectPresence() returns true
    // Used when no explicit agent is specified
}
```

**E2E Test Agent Import** (`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_cmd.go:5-8`):
```go
import _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
import _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"
import _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot"
```

Agents are imported via blank imports to trigger their `init()` functions.

---

### 7. E2E Test Infrastructure Details

**Binary Building** (`setup_test.go:32-52`):
- Built once in TestMain before any tests run
- Placed in temp directory with unique path per test run
- Added to PATH so hooks can find it via `entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg`, etc.
- Shared across all tests to avoid repeated builds

**Test Environment Isolation** (`testenv.go:47-65`):
- Each test gets its own temporary git repository
- Symlinks resolved on macOS (for `/var` → `/private/var`)
- Git configured with test user credentials
- GPG signing disabled for test commits

**Hook Execution** (`testenv.go:261-329` - `GitCommitWithShadowHooks`):
1. Stage files with go-git
2. Write commit message to `.git/COMMIT_EDITMSG`
3. Invoke: `entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg <file> message`
4. Read modified message (with Entire-Checkpoint trailer added)
5. Create commit with go-git
6. Invoke: `entire hooks git post-commit`

Both hooks are called via direct `exec.Command` with test env vars set.

**Environment Variables for Hooks** (`testenv.go:280-284`):
```go
prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
    "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
    "ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
    "ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
)
```

These allow hooks to find agent project directories in test repos.

---

### 8. Test Scenario Coverage

**Available Test Scenarios** (`scenario_*.go`):

| File | Tests | Coverage |
|------|-------|----------|
| `scenario_basic_workflow_test.go` | TestE2E_BasicWorkflow, TestE2E_MultipleChanges | Basic file creation, checkpoint creation, multi-step workflows |
| `scenario_checkpoint_test.go` | TestE2E_CheckpointMetadata, TestE2E_CheckpointIDFormat, ... | Checkpoint structure, ID format, metadata validation |
| `scenario_agent_commit_test.go` | TestE2E_AgentCommitsDuringTurn, TestE2E_MultipleAgentSessions | Agent-initiated commits, multi-session state |
| `scenario_rewind_test.go` | TestE2E_Rewind, TestE2E_RewindAfterNewChanges | Rewind functionality, state restoration |
| `scenario_subagent_test.go` | TestE2E_SubagentCheckpoint, TestE2E_SubagentCheckpoint_CommitFlow | Task tool usage, subagent checkpoints |
| `scenario_checkpoint_workflows_test.go` | Multiple tests (~1,300 lines) | Complex workflows, edge cases, condensation |

**Prompt Templates** (`prompts.go`):
- `PromptCreateHelloGo` - Simple Go hello world
- `PromptModifyHelloGo` - Modify existing file
- `PromptCreateCalculator` - Multi-function program
- `PromptCreateConfig` - JSON config file
- `PromptAddMultiplyFunction` - Add to existing file
- `PromptCreateDocs` - Documentation file
- `PromptCommitChanges` - Agent-initiated commits
- `PromptUseTaskTool` - Subagent delegation

All prompts are deterministic and designed for minimal token usage.

---

### 9. Assertion Helpers

**Available Helpers** (`assertions.go`):
- `AssertFileContains(t, env, path, expected)` - Check substring in file
- `AssertFileMatches(t, env, path, pattern)` - Regex match
- `AssertHelloWorldProgram(t, env, path)` - Verify Go program structure
- `AssertCalculatorFunctions(t, env, path, ...functions)` - Verify functions exist
- `AssertRewindPointExists(t, env)` - Check for any rewind point
- `AssertRewindPointCount(t, env, expected)` - Exact count check
- `AssertCheckpointExists(t, env)` - Verify checkpoint trailer in history
- `AssertBranchExists(t, env, branchName)` - Branch existence
- `AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)` - Agent exit code check
- `AssertExpectedFilesExist(t, env, prompt)` - Files from prompt template

---

### 10. Key Architectural Patterns

**1. Multi-Agent Support:**
- AgentRunner interface allows any agent to be tested
- E2E_AGENT env var selects which agent to test
- Each agent has its own Runner implementation
- Tests auto-skip if agent unavailable

**2. Hook Simulation:**
- Hooks are NOT simulated - they're actual CLI invocations
- Tests manually call `entire hooks git <hook>` like real git would
- This tests the actual hook implementation, not a mock

**3. Real Agent Invocation:**
- Tests invoke the actual agent CLI (e.g., `claude` command)
- No agent mocking or stubbing
- Requires agent to be installed and authenticated
- Timeout handling prevents hanging tests

**4. Checkpoint Validation:**
- `ValidateCheckpoint()` helper performs comprehensive validation
- Checks metadata branch structure (`entire/checkpoints/v1`)
- Verifies sharded directory format: `<id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/`
- Validates JSONL transcript format
- Checks content hashes match

**5. Session Lifecycle:**
- Tests manually trigger agent sessions via `RunAgent()`
- Rewind points available before commit
- Commit triggers hook-based checkpoint creation
- Multiple sessions supported in same directory

---

### 11. Running E2E Tests

**Command Line:**
```bash
# Default (claude-code agent)
E2E_AGENT=claude-code go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# With specific model
E2E_CLAUDE_MODEL=opus E2E_AGENT=claude-code go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# With timeout override
E2E_TIMEOUT=5m E2E_AGENT=claude-code go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# Specific test
E2E_AGENT=claude-code go test -tags=e2e -run TestE2E_BasicWorkflow ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# Gemini agent
E2E_AGENT=gemini go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...
```

**Agent Availability Check:**
- CLI must be installed and in PATH
- For Claude Code: requires `claude login` authentication
- For Gemini: requires Google authentication
- Tests gracefully skip (exit 0) if agent unavailable

---

### 12. Notable Implementation Details

**Symlink Resolution** (`testenv.go:52-54`):
```go
repoDir := t.TempDir()
if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    repoDir = resolved
}
```
Needed because macOS temp dirs resolve to `/var/` which is a symlink to `/private/var/`.

**Shadow Hook Execution** (`testenv.go:261-329`):
The `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` method manually executes git hooks because go-git doesn't automatically trigger them. This accurately simulates the real git commit workflow.

**Trailer Removal Testing** (`testenv.go:412-500` - `GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved`):
Tests can verify that users can opt-out of checkpoint linking by removing the `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer before committing.

**Checkpoint Metadata Structure:**
```
entire/checkpoints/v1 (orphan branch)
└── <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    ├── metadata.json (CheckpointSummary)
    ├── 0/ (first session)
    │   ├── metadata.json
    │   ├── full.jsonl (JSONL transcript)
    │   ├── prompt.txt
    │   ├── context.md
    │   ├── content_hash.txt
    │   └── tasks/<tool-use-id>/ (subagent checkpoints)
    │       ├── checkpoint.json
    │       └── agent-<id>.jsonl
    └── 1/ (second session, if multiple)
```

**Rewind Points:**
Returned as JSON from `entire rewind --list` and include:
- `id` - Checkpoint ID
- `message` - Session message
- `metadata_dir` - Path to metadata directory
- `date` - Creation time
- `is_task_checkpoint` - Whether it's a subagent checkpoint
- `tool_use_id` - Tool identifier (for task checkpoints)
- `is_logs_only` - Whether it's condensed metadata only
- `condensation_id` - ID from condensation (if applicable)

---

This comprehensive infrastructure allows thorough testing of the agent integration with real agent CLIs, real git operations, and real checkpoint creation workflows, while maintaining test isolation and avoiding expensive repeated builds.
