## Summary Report: GitHub Copilot CLI Agent Architecture & E2E Testing Requirements

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here's what I've discovered about how the GitHub Copilot CLI looks from the outside and how e2e tests should be structured:

---

### 1. **Copilot CLI Binary Invocation**

**Binary name**: `copilot` (command-line tool)

**Resume command** (from `FormatResumeCommand` in copilot.go):
```go
// Line 227-229 in copilot.go
func (c *CopilotAgent) FormatResumeCommand(_ string) string {
    return "copilot --resume"
}
```

So to resume a session: `copilot --resume`

**Other flags**: The codebase doesn't expose other CLI flags for Copilot yet, but based on the pattern with Claude Code and Gemini, the agent likely accepts:
- Prompts via stdin or `-p/--prompt` flag
- Tool restrictions via `--allowedTools` or similar
- Model selection (if multi-model)

---

### 2. **Hook Configuration & Invocation Pattern**

Copilot stores hooks in `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` (not like Claude's `.claude/settings.json`):

**Config structure** (from hooks.go and types in copilot.go):
```json
{
  "version": 1,
  "hooks": {
    "sessionStart": [
      {"type": "command", "bash": "entire hooks github-copilot session-start", "timeoutSec": 10}
    ],
    "agentStop": [
      {"type": "command", "bash": "entire hooks github-copilot agent-stop", "timeoutSec": 10}
    ],
    ...
  }
}
```

**Hook invocation pattern**:
```bash
entire hooks github-copilot <hook-subcommand>
```

Example hook subcommands:
- `session-start`
- `session-end`
- `user-prompt-submitted`
- `agent-stop`
- `subagent-stop`
- `pre-tool-use`
- `post-tool-use`
- `error-occurred`

**Hook stdin format** (from copilot.go ParseHookInput):
```json
{
  "sessionId": "string",
  "transcript_path": "/path/to/events.jsonl",
  "cwd": "/working/directory",
  "timestamp": "ISO8601",
  "hookEventName": "string",
  // Hook-specific fields...
  "prompt": "user prompt text",  // for userPromptSubmitted
  "reason": "completion|cancelled",  // for sessionEnd
  "toolName": "string",  // for pre/postToolUse
  "toolArgs": {...},
  "toolResult": {...}
}
```

---

### 3. **Session Storage & Transcript Format**

**Session directory**: `~/.copilot/session-state/` (overridable via `ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR` env var for testing)

**Session file path**: `~/.copilot/session-state/<sessionId>/events.jsonl`

**Transcript format**: JSONL (JSON Lines - one JSON object per line, like Claude Code)

**Sample session structure**:
```
~/.copilot/session-state/
└── <sessionId>/
    └── events.jsonl      # JSONL transcript
```

---

### 4. **Agent Detection & Configuration**

From `copilot.go` `DetectPresence()`:

**Detection logic**:
1. Check if hooks are installed (`AreHooksInstalled()` → looks for `entire` commands in `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`)
2. OR check for `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` file existence
3. Returns `true` if either is detected

**Agent name**: `"github-copilot"` (used in CLI: `--agent github-copilot` or `entire enable --agent github-copilot`)

**Agent type**: `"GitHub Copilot"` (display name in metadata/trailers)

**Preview status**: `IsPreview() = true` (marked as preview integration)

---

### 5. **Existing E2E Test Infrastructure Pattern**

From the e2e test files, here's how agents are tested:

**Test structure** (`e2e_test/agent_runner.go`):
```go
type AgentRunner interface {
    Name() string
    IsAvailable() (bool, error)
    RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error)
    RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error)
}
```

**Current implementations**:
1. **ClaudeCodeRunner**: Uses `exec.Command("claude", args...)` - invokes the `claude` binary
2. **GeminiCLIRunner**: Uses `exec.Command("gemini", args...)` - invokes the `gemini` binary

**Agent binary availability check** (from ClaudeCodeRunner.IsAvailable):
```go
// Check if claude CLI is in PATH
if _, err := exec.LookPath("claude"); err != nil {
    return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
}

// Check if claude is working (--version doesn't require auth)
ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
defer cancel()

cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--version")
if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
    return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not working: %w", err)
}

return true, nil
```

---

### 6. **E2E Test Flow with Agents**

From `e2e_test/testenv.go` and `setup_test.go`:

```go
// Step 1: TestMain() determines agent
defaultAgent = os.Getenv("E2E_AGENT")  // e.g., "claude-code", "gemini", or "github-copilot"
if defaultAgent == "" {
    defaultAgent = "claude-code"  // fallback
}

// Step 2: Check agent availability
runner := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
available, err := runner.IsAvailable()
if !available {
    fmt.Printf("Agent %s not available, skipping E2E tests\n", defaultAgent)
    os.Exit(0)  // Exit 0 = skip gracefully
}

// Step 3: Build CLI binary once
testBinaryPath = filepath.Join(tmpDir, "entire")
buildCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "go", "build", "-o", testBinaryPath, ".")
buildCmd.Dir = filepath.Join(moduleRoot, "cmd", "entire")

// Step 4: Tests call env.RunAgent(prompt) → runner.RunPrompt()
result, err := env.Agent.RunPrompt(context.Background(), env.RepoDir, prompt)
```

---

### 7. **Hook Integration in Tests**

Tests use `GitCommitWithShadowHooks()` to simulate hook execution:

```go
// From e2e_test/testenv.go (lines 261-329)
func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
    // 1. Stage files using go-git
    for _, file := range files {
        env.GitAdd(file)
    }
    
    // 2. Run prepare-commit-msg hook
    prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
    
    // 3. Create commit using go-git
    worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), ...)
    
    // 4. Run post-commit hook
    postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
    if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
        env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
    }
}
```

For agent hooks, tests would call:
```go
exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "github-copilot", "agent-stop")
```

---

### 8. **Key Requirements for Copilot E2E Testing**

**Required for e2e tests to work**:

1. **Copilot CLI binary** must be installed and in `PATH`
   - Checked via `exec.LookPath("copilot")`
   - Verified with `copilot --version`

2. **Copilot authentication** must be valid
   - User must have run `copilot login` previously
   - Session file directory (`~/.copilot/session-state/`) must be writable

3. **Hook stdin simulation** for testing without real agent
   - Create mock JSON for each hook and pipe to `entire hooks github-copilot <subcommand>`
   - Or run real `copilot` agent and capture actual stdin

4. **Integration test environment variables**:
   - `ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR` - override `~/.copilot/session-state/` for testing
   - `E2E_AGENT=github-copilot` - run e2e tests with Copilot
   - `E2E_TIMEOUT` - increase for slow Copilot responses

---

### 9. **Critical Differences from Claude Code**

| Aspect | Claude Code | Copilot | Impact |
|--------|------------|---------|--------|
| **Config location** | `.claude/settings.json` | `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` | Different config file paths |
| **Session storage** | `~/.claude/projects/<sanitized-path>/` | `~/.copilot/session-state/` | Different env var overrides needed |
| **Session ID source** | Hook provides directly | Hook provides directly | Same pattern |
| **Hooks in git** | Via `.claude/settings.json` | Via `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` | Different git tracking |
| **Protected dirs** | `.claude` must be protected | None (`.github` is shared) | Rewind operations differ |
| **Resume command** | `claude -r <sessionId>` | `copilot --resume` | Different CLI invocation |
| **Hook stdin format** | Claude's format | Copilot's format (common fields) | Different JSON parsing |

---

### 10. **Files Implementing Copilot Agent**

Already committed to branch:
- `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/copilot.go` - Core agent impl
- `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/lifecycle.go` - Hook event parsing
- `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/hooks.go` - Hook install/uninstall
- `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/types.go` - Type definitions

- `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/transcript_test.go` - Transcript tests

---

### 11. **How to Add Copilot to E2E Tests**

To run e2e tests with Copilot:

```bash
# Step 1: Install copilot CLI
# (User must do this themselves or via CI setup)

# Step 2: Add CopilotRunner to e2e_test/agent_runner.go
type CopilotRunner struct {
    Model        string
    Timeout      time.Duration
    AllowedTools []string
}

// Step 3: Update NewAgentRunner factory
func NewAgentRunner(name string, config AgentRunnerConfig) AgentRunner {
    switch name {
    case AgentNameClaudeCode:
        return NewClaudeCodeRunner(config)
    case AgentNameGemini:
        return NewGeminiCLIRunner(config)
    case "github-copilot":  // NEW
        return NewCopilotRunner(config)
    default:
        return &unavailableRunner{name: name}
    }
}

// Step 4: Run tests
E2E_AGENT=github-copilot go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...
```

The `CopilotRunner.IsAvailable()` would check:
1. `exec.LookPath("copilot")` succeeds
2. `copilot --version` runs successfully
3. `~/.copilot/session-state/` directory is writable (or env override exists)

---

### Summary

The GitHub Copilot CLI integration is **functionally complete** at the agent interface level. All hooks are implemented, event parsing works, and transcript handling is in place. The only missing piece for **e2e testing** is:

1. **CopilotRunner** implementation in `e2e_test/agent_runner.go` - a thin wrapper around `exec.Command("copilot", ...)`
2. **Environment variable defaults** for test overrides (`ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR`)
3. **Hook stdin fixtures** or mock data to test agent interactions without a real Copilot session

The pattern is identical to Claude Code and Gemini: detect the binary, verify it works, then invoke it with a prompt and capture output.

## Copilot Agent Integration - Comprehensive Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a detailed breakdown of how the GitHub Copilot agent integrates with Entire:

### 1. **Registry Registration** (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go`)

The copilot agent is registered as:
- **Registry Name (AgentName)**: `"github-copilot"`
- **Type Name (AgentType)**: `"GitHub Copilot"`
- **Self-registration**: In `cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/copilot.go`, the `init()` function calls:
  ```go
  agent.Register(agent.AgentNameCopilot, NewCopilotAgent)
  ```

The registry uses a factory pattern with a map-based lookup:
```go
var registry = make(map[AgentName]Factory)  // Factory = func() Agent
```

### 2. **Core Copilot Agent Structure** (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/copilot.go`)

**The `CopilotAgent` struct**: Empty struct that implements the `Agent` interface
```go
type CopilotAgent struct{}

func NewCopilotAgent() agent.Agent {
    return &CopilotAgent{}
}
```

**Identity Methods**:
- `Name()` → `agent.AgentNameCopilot` (`"github-copilot"`)
- `Type()` → `agent.AgentTypeCopilot` (`"GitHub Copilot"`)
- `Description()` → `"GitHub Copilot - GitHub's AI coding assistant"`
- `IsPreview()` → `true` (returns true, marking it as preview)
- `ProtectedDirs()` → `[]string{}` (empty - `.github` is shared, not copilot-specific)

**Detection** (`DetectPresence()`):
- Checks if hooks are installed via `AreHooksInstalled()`
- OR checks for `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` file existence
- Falls back to cwd if `RepoRoot()` fails

**Hook Configuration**:
- `GetHookConfigPath()` → `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`
- `SupportsHooks()` → `true`

### 3. **Session Storage Methods**

**`GetSessionDir(repoPath string)`**:
- Returns: `~/.copilot/session-state/` (Copilot's native session storage)
- Respects override: `ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR` environment variable for testing
- Uses `os.UserHomeDir()` to locate home directory

**`ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID)`**:
- Returns: `filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID, "events.jsonl")`
- Full path example: `~/.copilot/session-state/{sessionID}/events.jsonl`

**`FormatResumeCommand(sessionID)`**:
- Returns: `"copilot --resume"` (simple resume command)
- Note: Doesn't use the sessionID parameter (unlike some agents that include it)

**Session I/O**:
- `ReadSession(input *HookInput)` → reads from `input.SessionRef` file path
- `WriteSession(session *AgentSession)` → writes to `session.SessionRef` with 0o600 permissions
- Stores raw JSONL bytes in `session.NativeData`

### 4. **Interfaces Implemented**

**Core Agent Interface** (always):
```go
type Agent interface {
    // Identity (5 methods)
    Name() AgentName
    Type() AgentType
    Description() string
    IsPreview() bool
    DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    ProtectedDirs() []string
    
    // Event Mapping (2 methods)
    HookNames() []string
    ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    
    // Transcript Storage (3 methods)
    ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    
    // Legacy methods (8 methods) - Will be moved to optional interfaces
    GetHookConfigPath() string
    SupportsHooks() bool
    ParseHookInput(...) (*HookInput, error)
    GetSessionID(*HookInput) string
    GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
    ReadSession(*HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    WriteSession(*AgentSession) error
    FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
}
```

**HookSupport Interface** (implemented):
```go
type HookSupport interface {
    Agent
    InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
    UninstallHooks() error
    AreHooksInstalled() bool
    GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
}
```

**HookHandler Interface** (implemented):
```go
type HookHandler interface {
    Agent
    GetHookNames() []string  // Now part of core Agent, kept for backward compat
}
```

**TranscriptAnalyzer Interface** (implemented):
- `GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)` - line count in JSONL
- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, offset int) (files []string, position int, error)`
- `ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)`
- `ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)`

### 5. **Hook Support** (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/hooks.go`)

**8 Hooks Supported**:
1. `session-start` → `sessionStart` (config key) - `SessionStart` event
2. `session-end` → `sessionEnd` - `SessionEnd` event
3. `user-prompt-submitted` → `userPromptSubmitted` - `TurnStart` event
4. `agent-stop` → `agentStop` - `TurnEnd` event
5. `subagent-stop` → `subagentStop` - `SubagentEnd` event
6. `pre-tool-use` → `preToolUse` - pass-through (nil event)
7. `post-tool-use` → `postToolUse` - pass-through (nil event)
8. `error-occurred` → `errorOccurred` - pass-through (nil event)

**Hook Installation** (`InstallHooks(localDev, force)`):
1. Reads existing `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` or creates new
2. Maps CLI subcommand names to Copilot's camelCase hook keys
3. Checks idempotency - if already installed with same mode, returns 0
4. Removes existing Entire hooks (can be forced)
5. Adds 8 new hook entries with command format:
   ```
   "entire hooks github-copilot <hook-name>"  (or with go run for localDev)
   ```
6. Creates `.github/hooks/` directory if needed
7. Writes JSON with indentation
8. Returns count of hooks installed (8)

**Hook Detection** (`AreHooksInstalled()`):
- Parses `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`
- Checks if any entry matches Entire hook prefixes:
  - `"entire "`
  - `"go run ${COPILOT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go "`

### 6. **`entire enable --agent copilot` Flow** (`cmd/entire/cli/setup.go`)

**Entry Point**: `newEnableCmd()` with `--agent copilot` flag

**Flow**:
1. **Prerequisite checks**:
   - Validates we're in a git repository
   - Validates flags (no `--local` and `--project` together)

2. **Non-interactive agent setup** (triggered by `--agent` flag):
   ```go
   setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(cmd.OutOrStdout(), ag, strategyFlag, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
   ```

3. **In `setupAgentHooksNonInteractive()`**:
   - Gets agent (cast to `HookSupport` interface)
   - Calls `hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)` → installs 8 hooks
   - Calls `setupEntireDirectory()` → creates `.entire/` and `.gitignore`
   - Loads existing settings (preserves options like `push_sessions`)
   - Sets `Enabled = true` and strategy name
   - Saves settings to `.entire/settings.json` or `.entire/settings.local.json`
   - Calls `strategy.InstallGitHook(true)` → installs git hooks
   - Calls `strat.EnsureSetup()` → strategy-specific setup (e.g., creates `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch)

4. **Output**:
   ```
   Agent: GitHub Copilot
   
   Installed 8 hooks for GitHub Copilot - GitHub's AI coding assistant (Preview)
   ✓ Project configured (.entire/settings.json)
   
   Ready.
   ```

**Agent Detection** (auto-detection in interactive mode):
- Calls `agent.DetectAll()` which checks all registered agents' `DetectPresence()` methods
- Copilot detected if:
  - `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` with Entire hooks exists, OR
  - Hooks are already installed

### 7. **Hook Dispatch Flow** (`cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry.go`)

**Command Structure**:
```
entire hooks
├── git (strategy-level hooks)
└── github-copilot (agent-specific hooks)
    ├── session-start
    ├── session-end
    ├── user-prompt-submitted
    ├── agent-stop
    ├── subagent-stop
    ├── pre-tool-use
    ├── post-tool-use
    └── error-occurred
```

**Hook Execution** (`newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging`):
1. **Early exits**:
   - Returns nil if not in a git repo
   - Returns nil if Entire is disabled

2. **Logging context** set up with agent name

3. **Hook parsing** via `agent.ParseHookEvent(hookName, stdin)`:
   - Copilot agent's `ParseHookEvent()` implementation in `lifecycle.go`
   - Reads and parses JSON from stdin
   - Returns normalized `Event` or `nil` (for pass-through hooks)

4. **Event dispatch** (if event != nil):
   - Calls `DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)`
   - Handles lifecycle state transitions and checkpoint creation

5. **Special handling**:
   - PostTodo (Claude-specific) is handled separately
   - Other pass-through hooks (nil event) are no-ops

6. **Logging** of duration and success

### 8. **Hook Input Types** (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/types.go`)

All Copilot hooks include common base fields (JSON from stdin):
```go
type hookBase struct {
    Timestamp int64  `json:"timestamp"`
    Cwd       string `json:"cwd"`
}
```

**Hook-specific types**:
- `sessionStartRaw`: base + Source, InitialPrompt
- `sessionEndRaw`: base + Reason
- `userPromptRaw`: base + Prompt
- `agentStopRaw`: base + SessionID, TranscriptPath, StopReason
- `subagentStopRaw`: base + SessionID, TranscriptPath
- `toolUseRaw`: base + ToolName, ToolArgs (JSON string in pre, object in post), ToolResult

**Event parsing** uses generic helper:
```go
agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[T](stdin) // T = hookBase subtype
```

### 9. **Comparison: Copilot vs Claude Code**

| Aspect | Copilot | Claude Code |
|--------|---------|-------------|
| **Hook Config Location** | `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json` | `.claude/settings.json` (JSON object matcher syntax) |
| **Session Storage** | `~/.copilot/session-state/{id}/` | `~/.claude/sessions/{id}/` |
| **Transcript Format** | JSONL (`events.jsonl`) | JSONL |
| **Hook Count** | 8 hooks | More hooks (with pre/post task) |
| **Preview Status** | Yes (`IsPreview() = true`) | No (stable) |
| **Config Key Format** | camelCase (`sessionStart`) | camelCase matchers in array |
| **Resume Command** | `copilot --resume` | `claude --resume` |
| **Protected Dirs** | None (`.github` is shared) | `.claude` (isolated to Claude) |
| **File Modification Detection** | Via `tool.execution_start` events | Via `write`/`edit` tool calls |

### 10. **Key Implementation Details**

**Idempotent Hook Installation**:
```go
// Check if already installed with same mode
if !force {
    expectedCmd := cmdPrefix + HookNameSessionStart
    if entries exist && match expectedCmd {
        return 0, nil  // Already installed
    }
}
// Remove and reinstall if needed
```

**Copilot's Events Format** (inferred from code):
```json
{
  "type": "user.message|assistant.message|tool.execution_start",
  "data": {...},
  "id": "uuid",
  "timestamp": "iso8601",
  "parentId": "uuid|null"
}
```

**File Extraction** from Copilot events:
- Looks for `tool.execution_start` events
- Checks tool names: `edit`, `create`
- Extracts file path from arguments: `path`, `file_path`, or `filePath` fields

**Prompt Extraction**:
- Finds `user.message` events
- Extracts `content` field (not `transformedContent` which includes injected context)

**Summary Extraction**:
- Searches backwards from end of JSONL
- Finds last `assistant.message` event with non-empty content

---

This comprehensive integration allows Entire to track GitHub Copilot CLI sessions alongside Claude Code and Gemini CLI, with consistent checkpoint and rewind functionality across all three agents.

tool_uses: 24
duration_ms: 63975</usage>
```

    22→// AgentRunner abstracts invoking a coding agent for e2e tests.
    23→// This follows the multi-agent pattern from cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go.
    24→type AgentRunner interface {
    25→	// Name returns the agent name (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini-cli")
    26→	Name() string
    27→
    28→	// IsAvailable checks if the agent CLI is installed and authenticated
    29→	IsAvailable() (bool, error)
    30→
    31→	// RunPrompt executes a prompt and returns the result

    33→
    34→	// RunPromptWithTools executes with specific allowed tools
    35→	RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// AgentResult holds the result of an agent invocation.
    39→type AgentResult struct {
    40→	Stdout   string
    41→	Stderr   string
    42→	ExitCode int
    43→	Duration time.Duration
    44→}
    45→
    46→// AgentRunnerConfig holds configuration for agent runners.

    49→	Timeout time.Duration // Timeout per prompt
    50→}
    51→
    52→// NewAgentRunner creates an agent runner based on the agent name.
    53→//
    54→//nolint:ireturn // factory pattern intentionally returns interface
    55→func NewAgentRunner(name string, config AgentRunnerConfig) AgentRunner {
    56→	switch name {
    57→	case AgentNameClaudeCode:
    58→		return NewClaudeCodeRunner(config)
    59→	case AgentNameGemini:
    60→		return NewGeminiCLIRunner(config)
    61→	default:
    62→		// Return a runner that reports as unavailable
    63→		return &unavailableRunner{name: name}
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→

    73→

    76→}
    77→

    86→// ClaudeCodeRunner implements AgentRunner for Claude Code CLI.
    87→type ClaudeCodeRunner struct {
    88→	Model        string

    92→
    93→// NewClaudeCodeRunner creates a new Claude Code runner with the given config.
    94→func NewClaudeCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *ClaudeCodeRunner {
    95→	model := config.Model
    96→	if model == "" {
    97→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_CLAUDE_MODEL")
    98→		if model == "" {
    99→			model = "haiku"
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	timeout := config.Timeout
   104→	if timeout == 0 {
   105→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   106→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   107→				timeout = parsed
   108→			}
   109→		}
   110→		if timeout == 0 {
   111→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   112→		}
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	return &ClaudeCodeRunner{
   116→		Model:        model,
   117→		Timeout:      timeout,
   118→		AllowedTools: []string{"Edit", "Read", "Write", "Bash", "Glob", "Grep"},
   119→	}
   120→}
   121→
   122→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) Name() string {
   123→	return AgentNameClaudeCode
   124→}
   125→
   126→// IsAvailable checks if Claude CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   127→// Note: This does NOT verify authentication status. Claude Code uses OAuth
   128→// authentication (via `claude login`), not ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. If the CLI is
   129→// installed but not logged in, tests will fail at RunPrompt time.
   130→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   131→	// Check if claude CLI is in PATH
   132→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("claude"); err != nil {
   133→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Check if claude is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   137→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   138→	defer cancel()
   139→
   140→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--version")
   141→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   142→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not working: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	return true, nil
   146→}
   147→
   148→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   149→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, r.AllowedTools)
   150→}
   151→
   152→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   153→	// Build command: claude --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allowedTools <tools>
   154→	args := []string{
   155→		"--model", r.Model,
   156→		"-p", prompt,
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	if len(tools) > 0 {
   160→		// Claude CLI expects each tool as a separate argument after --allowedTools
   161→		// e.g., --allowedTools Edit Read Bash (not --allowedTools "Edit,Read,Bash")
   162→		args = append(args, "--allowedTools")
   163→		args = append(args, tools...)
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Create context with timeout
   167→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   168→	defer cancel()
   169→
   170→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   171→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", args...)
   172→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   173→
   174→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   175→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   176→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   177→
   178→	start := time.Now()
   179→	err := cmd.Run()
   180→	duration := time.Since(start)
   181→
   182→	result := &AgentResult{
   183→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   184→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   185→		Duration: duration,
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   190→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   191→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   192→		} else {
   193→			result.ExitCode = -1
   194→		}
   195→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   196→		return result, err
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	result.ExitCode = 0
   200→	return result, nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// GeminiCLIRunner implements AgentRunner for Gemini CLI.
   204→// See: https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli
   205→type GeminiCLIRunner struct {
   206→	Model   string
   207→	Timeout time.Duration
   208→}
   209→
   210→// NewGeminiCLIRunner creates a new Gemini CLI runner with the given config.
   211→func NewGeminiCLIRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *GeminiCLIRunner {
   212→	model := config.Model
   213→	if model == "" {
   214→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_GEMINI_MODEL")
   215→		// Default to gemini-2.5-flash if not specified
   216→		if model == "" {
   217→			model = "gemini-2.5-flash"
   218→		}
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	timeout := config.Timeout
   222→	if timeout == 0 {
   223→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   224→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   225→				timeout = parsed
   226→			}
   227→		}
   228→		if timeout == 0 {
   229→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return &GeminiCLIRunner{
   234→		Model:   model,
   235→		Timeout: timeout,
   236→	}
   237→}
   238→
   239→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) Name() string {
   240→	return AgentNameGemini
   241→}
   242→
   243→// IsAvailable checks if Gemini CLI is installed and authenticated.
   244→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   245→	// Check if gemini CLI is in PATH
   246→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("gemini"); err != nil {
   247→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Check if gemini is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   251→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   252→	defer cancel()
   253→
   254→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", "--version")
   255→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   256→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not working: %w", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return true, nil
   260→}
   261→
   262→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   263→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
   264→}
   265→
   266→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   267→	// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p "<prompt>" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>
   268→	// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others
   269→	// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)
   270→	args := []string{
   271→		"-m", r.Model,
   272→		"-p", prompt,
   273→		"--approval-mode", "auto_edit",
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation
   277→	defaultAllowedTools := []string{
   278→		"ShellTool(git status)",
   279→		"ShellTool(git add)",
   280→		"ShellTool(git commit)",
   281→		"ShellTool(git diff)",
   282→		"ShellTool(git log)",
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// Merge with any additional tools passed in
   286→	allTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)
   287→	if len(allTools) > 0 {
   288→		args = append(args, "--allowed-tools", strings.Join(allTools, ","))
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Create context with timeout
   292→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   293→	defer cancel()
   294→
   295→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   296→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", args...)
   297→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   298→
   299→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   300→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   301→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   302→
   303→	start := time.Now()
   304→	err := cmd.Run()
   305→	duration := time.Since(start)
   306→
   307→	result := &AgentResult{
   308→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   309→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   310→		Duration: duration,
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   315→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   316→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   317→		} else {
   318→			result.ExitCode = -1
   319→		}
   320→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   321→		return result, err
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	result.ExitCode = 0
   325→	return result, nil
   326→}
   327→

     1→//go:build e2e
     2→
     3→package e2e
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"runtime"
    12→	"testing"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// TestMain builds the CLI binary once and checks agent availability before running tests.
    16→func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
    17→	// Determine which agent to test with
    18→	defaultAgent = os.Getenv("E2E_AGENT")
    19→	if defaultAgent == "" {
    20→		defaultAgent = AgentNameClaudeCode
    21→	}
    22→
    23→	// Check if the agent is available
    24→	runner := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
    25→	available, err := runner.IsAvailable()
    26→	if !available {
    27→		fmt.Printf("Agent %s not available (%v), skipping E2E tests\n", defaultAgent, err)
    28→		// Exit 0 to not fail CI when agent isn't configured
    29→		os.Exit(0)
    30→	}
    31→
    32→	// Build binary once to a temp directory
    33→	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "entire-e2e-test-*")
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "failed to create temp dir for binary: %v\n", err)
    36→		os.Exit(1)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	testBinaryPath = filepath.Join(tmpDir, "entire")
    40→
    41→	moduleRoot := findModuleRoot()
    42→	ctx := context.Background()
    43→
    44→	buildCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "go", "build", "-o", testBinaryPath, ".")
    45→	buildCmd.Dir = filepath.Join(moduleRoot, "cmd", "entire")
    46→
    47→	buildOutput, err := buildCmd.CombinedOutput()
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "failed to build CLI binary: %v\nOutput: %s\n", err, buildOutput)
    50→		os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
    51→		os.Exit(1)
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	// Add binary to PATH so hooks can find it.
    55→	// This is safe because:
    56→	// 1. TestMain runs once before any tests (no parallel conflict with os.Setenv)
    57→	// 2. Each test package gets its own TestMain execution
    58→	// 3. PATH is restored in the same TestMain after m.Run() completes
    59→	// 4. The binary path is unique per test run (temp dir)
    60→	origPath := os.Getenv("PATH")
    61→	os.Setenv("PATH", tmpDir+string(os.PathListSeparator)+origPath)
    62→
    63→	// Run tests
    64→	code := m.Run()
    65→
    66→	// Cleanup
    67→	os.Setenv("PATH", origPath)
    68→	os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
    69→	os.Exit(code)
    70→}
    71→
    72→// findModuleRoot finds the Go module root by walking up from the current file.
    73→func findModuleRoot() string {
    74→	_, thisFile, _, ok := runtime.Caller(0)
    75→	if !ok {
    76→		panic("failed to get current file path via runtime.Caller")
    77→	}
    78→	dir := filepath.Dir(thisFile)
    79→
    80→	for {
    81→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, "go.mod")); err == nil {
    82→			return dir
    83→		}
    84→		parent := filepath.Dir(dir)
    85→		if parent == dir {
    86→			panic("could not find go.mod starting from " + thisFile)
    87→		}
    88→		dir = parent
    89→	}
    90→}
    91→

     1→//go:build e2e
     2→
     3→package e2e
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"crypto/sha256"

    10→	"errors"

    21→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// defaultAgent holds the agent to test with, determined in TestMain.
    25→var defaultAgent string
    26→
    27→// testBinaryPath holds the path to the CLI binary built once in TestMain.
    28→// All tests share this binary to avoid repeated builds.
    29→var testBinaryPath string
    30→
    31→// TestEnv manages an isolated test environment for E2E tests with real agent calls.
    32→type TestEnv struct {
    33→	T       *testing.T
    34→	RepoDir string
    35→	Agent   AgentRunner
    36→}
    37→
    38→// getTestBinary returns the path to the shared test binary.
    39→// It panics if TestMain hasn't run (testBinaryPath is empty).
    40→func getTestBinary() string {
    41→	if testBinaryPath == "" {
    42→		panic("testBinaryPath not set - TestMain must run before tests")
    43→	}
    44→	return testBinaryPath
    45→}
    46→
    47→// NewTestEnv creates a new isolated E2E test environment.
    48→func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    49→	t.Helper()
    50→
    51→	// Resolve symlinks on macOS where /var -> /private/var
    52→	repoDir := t.TempDir()
    53→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    54→		repoDir = resolved
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Create agent runner
    58→	agent := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
    59→
    60→	return &TestEnv{
    61→		T:       t,
    62→		RepoDir: repoDir,
    63→		Agent:   agent,
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates an E2E test environment ready for testing.
    68→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,
    69→// checks out a feature branch, and sets up agent hooks.
    70→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {
    71→	t.Helper()
    72→
    73→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    74→	env.InitRepo()
    75→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository\n\nThis is a test repository for E2E testing.\n")
    76→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    77→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    78→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/e2e-test")
    79→
    80→	// Use `entire enable` to set up everything (hooks, settings, etc.)
    81→	// This sets up .entire/settings.json and .claude/settings.json with hooks
    82→	env.RunEntireEnable(strategyName)
    83→
    84→	// Commit all files created by `entire enable` so they survive git stash -u operations.
    85→	// Without this, stash operations would stash away the hooks config and entire settings,
    86→	// causing hooks to not fire for subsequent prompts and stash pop conflicts.
    87→	// Using "git add ." is safe in test repos since we control all content.
    88→	env.GitAddAll()
    89→	env.GitCommit("Add entire and agent config")
    90→
    91→	return env
    92→}
    93→
    94→// RunEntireEnable runs `entire enable` to set up the project with hooks.
    95→// Uses the configured defaultAgent (from E2E_AGENT env var or "claude-code").
    96→func (env *TestEnv) RunEntireEnable(strategyName string) {
    97→	env.T.Helper()
    98→
    99→	args := []string{
   100→		"enable",
   101→		"--agent", defaultAgent,
   102→		"--strategy", strategyName,
   103→		"--telemetry=false",
   104→		"--force", // Force reinstall hooks in case they exist
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static
   108→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), args...)
   109→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   110→
   111→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		env.T.Fatalf("entire enable failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   114→	}
   115→	env.T.Logf("entire enable output: %s", output)
   116→}

   118→// InitRepo initializes a git repository in the test environment.
   119→func (env *TestEnv) InitRepo() {
   120→	env.T.Helper()
   121→
   122→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(env.RepoDir, false)
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	// Configure git user for commits
   128→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get repo config: %v", err)
   131→	}
   132→	cfg.User.Name = "E2E Test User"
   133→	cfg.User.Email = "e2e-test@example.com"
   134→
   135→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits
   136→	if cfg.Raw == nil {
   137→		cfg.Raw = config.New()
   138→	}
   139→	cfg.Raw.Section("commit").SetOption("gpgsign", "false")
   140→
   141→	if err := repo.SetConfig(cfg); err != nil {
   142→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to set repo config: %v", err)
   143→	}
   144→}

   146→// WriteFile creates a file with the given content in the test repo.
   147→func (env *TestEnv) WriteFile(path, content string) {
   148→	env.T.Helper()
   149→
   150→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)
   151→
   152→	// Create parent directories
   153→	dir := filepath.Dir(fullPath)
   154→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   155→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   156→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create directory %s: %v", dir, err)
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   160→	if err := os.WriteFile(fullPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   161→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write file %s: %v", path, err)
   162→	}
   163→}

   259→// GitCommitWithShadowHooks stages and commits files, running the prepare-commit-msg
   260→// and post-commit hooks like a real workflow.
   261→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   262→	env.T.Helper()
   263→
   264→	// Stage files using go-git
   265→	for _, file := range files {
   266→		env.GitAdd(file)
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   270→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   271→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   272→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   273→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   277→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   278→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   279→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   280→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   281→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   282→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   283→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   284→	)
   285→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   286→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	// Read the modified message
   290→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   291→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   297→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   298→	if err != nil {
   299→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   303→	if err != nil {
   304→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   305→	}
   306→
   307→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   308→		Author: &object.Signature{
   309→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   310→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   311→			When:  time.Now(),
   312→		},
   313→	})
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Run post-commit hook
   319→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   320→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   321→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   322→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   323→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   324→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   325→	)
   326→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   327→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   328→	}
   329→}

   412→// GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved stages and commits files, simulating what happens when
   413→// a user removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer during commit message editing.
   414→// This tests the opt-out behavior where removing the trailer skips condensation.
   415→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved(message string, files ...string) {
   416→	env.T.Helper()
   417→
   418→	// Stage files using go-git
   419→	for _, file := range files {
   420→		env.GitAdd(file)
   421→	}
   422→
   423→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   424→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   425→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   426→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   427→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   428→	}
   429→
   430→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   431→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   432→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
   433→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   434→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   435→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   436→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   437→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   438→	)
   439→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   440→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	// Read the modified message (with trailer added by hook)
   444→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   445→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	// REMOVE the Entire-Checkpoint trailer (simulating user editing the message)
   451→	lines := strings.Split(string(modifiedMsg), "\n")
   452→	var cleanedLines []string
   453→	for _, line := range lines {
   454→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   455→			continue
   456→		}
   457→		if strings.Contains(line, "Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer") {
   458→			continue
   459→		}
   460→		if strings.Contains(line, "trailer will be added to your next commit") {
   461→			continue
   462→		}
   463→		cleanedLines = append(cleanedLines, line)
   464→	}
   465→	cleanedMsg := strings.TrimRight(strings.Join(cleanedLines, "\n"), "\n") + "\n"
   466→
   467→	// Create the commit using go-git with the cleaned message (no trailer)
   468→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   469→	if err != nil {
   470→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   474→	if err != nil {
   475→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   476→	}
   477→
   478→	_, err = worktree.Commit(cleanedMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   479→		Author: &object.Signature{
   480→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   481→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   482→			When:  time.Now(),
   483→		},
   484→	})
   485→	if err != nil {
   486→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// Run post-commit hook - since trailer was removed, no condensation should happen
   490→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   491→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   492→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   493→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   494→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   495→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   496→	)
   497→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   498→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   499→	}
   500→}

   530→
   531→// RewindPoint mirrors the rewind --list JSON output.
   532→type RewindPoint struct {
   533→	ID               string    `json:"id"`
   534→	Message          string    `json:"message"`
   535→	MetadataDir      string    `json:"metadata_dir"`
   536→	Date             time.Time `json:"date"`
   537→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool      `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   538→	ToolUseID        string    `json:"tool_use_id"`
   539→	IsLogsOnly       bool      `json:"is_logs_only"`
   540→	CondensationID   string    `json:"condensation_id"`
   541→}
   542→
   543→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points using the CLI.
   544→func (env *TestEnv) GetRewindPoints() []RewindPoint {
   545→	env.T.Helper()
   546→
   547→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static, no context needed
   548→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--list")
   549→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   550→
   551→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		env.T.Fatalf("rewind --list failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Parse JSON output
   557→	var jsonPoints []struct {
   558→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   559→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   560→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   561→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   562→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   563→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   564→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   565→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id"`
   566→	}
   567→
   568→	if err := json.Unmarshal(output, &jsonPoints); err != nil {
   569→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to parse rewind points: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	points := make([]RewindPoint, len(jsonPoints))
   573→	for i, jp := range jsonPoints {
   574→		//nolint:errcheck // date parsing failure is acceptable, defaults to zero time
   575→		date, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, jp.Date)
   576→		points[i] = RewindPoint{
   577→			ID:               jp.ID,
   578→			Message:          jp.Message,
   579→			MetadataDir:      jp.MetadataDir,
   580→			Date:             date,
   581→			IsTaskCheckpoint: jp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   582→			ToolUseID:        jp.ToolUseID,
   583→			IsLogsOnly:       jp.IsLogsOnly,
   584→			CondensationID:   jp.CondensationID,
   585→		}
   586→	}
   587→
   588→	return points
   589→}
   590→
   591→// Rewind performs a rewind to the specified commit ID using the CLI.
   592→func (env *TestEnv) Rewind(commitID string) error {
   593→	env.T.Helper()
   594→
   595→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, commitID is from test setup, no context needed
   596→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--to", commitID)
   597→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir

   604→	env.T.Logf("Rewind output: %s", output)

   679→// GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns
   680→// the checkpoint ID from the first commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   681→// Returns an error if no checkpoint trailer is found in any commit.
   682→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory() (string, error) {
   683→	env.T.Helper()
   684→
   685→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   686→	if err != nil {
   687→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	head, err := repo.Head()
   691→	if err != nil {
   692→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   693→	}
   694→
   695→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   696→	if err != nil {
   697→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	var checkpointID string
   701→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback returns error to stop iteration, not a real error
   702→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   703→		// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   704→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   705→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   706→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   707→				checkpointID = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   708→				return errors.New("stop iteration")
   709→			}
   710→		}
   711→		return nil
   712→	})
   713→
   714→	if checkpointID == "" {
   715→		return "", errors.New("no commit with Entire-Checkpoint trailer found in history")
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	return checkpointID, nil
   719→}

   721→// GetLatestCheckpointID returns the checkpoint ID from HEAD commit, or empty string if none.
   722→// This is a convenience wrapper that doesn't fail the test on missing checkpoint.
   723→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointID() string {
   724→	env.T.Helper()
   725→
   726→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   727→	if err != nil {
   728→		return ""
   729→	}
   730→
   731→	head, err := repo.Head()
   732→	if err != nil {
   733→		return ""
   734→	}
   735→
   736→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   737→	if err != nil {
   738→		return ""
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer in HEAD commit only
   742→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
   743→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   744→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   745→			return strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   746→		}
   747→	}
   748→	return ""
   749→}

   757→	return id

   928→// ReadFileFromBranch reads a file's content from a specific branch's tree.
   929→// Returns the content and true if found, empty string and false if not found.
   930→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFileFromBranch(branchName, filePath string) (string, bool) {
   931→	env.T.Helper()
   932→
   933→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   934→	if err != nil {
   935→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   936→	}
   937→
   938→	// Get the branch reference
   939→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   940→	if err != nil {
   941→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   942→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   943→		if err != nil {
   944→			return "", false
   945→		}
   946→	}
   947→
   948→	// Get the commit
   949→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   950→	if err != nil {
   951→		return "", false
   952→	}
   953→
   954→	// Get the tree
   955→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   956→	if err != nil {
   957→		return "", false
   958→	}
   959→
   960→	// Get the file
   961→	file, err := tree.File(filePath)
   962→	if err != nil {
   963→		return "", false
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Get the content
   967→	content, err := file.Contents()
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		return "", false
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	return content, true
   973→}
   974→
   975→// CheckpointValidation contains expected values for checkpoint validation.
   976→type CheckpointValidation struct {
   977→	// CheckpointID is the expected checkpoint ID
   978→	CheckpointID string
   979→
   980→	// Strategy is the expected strategy name (optional)
   981→	Strategy string
   982→
   983→	// FilesTouched are the expected files in files_touched (optional)
   984→	FilesTouched []string
   985→
   986→	// ExpectedPrompts are strings that should appear in prompt.txt (optional)
   987→	ExpectedPrompts []string
   988→
   989→	// ExpectedTranscriptContent are strings that should appear in full.jsonl (optional)
   990→	ExpectedTranscriptContent []string
   991→}
   992→
   993→// ValidateCheckpoint performs comprehensive validation of a checkpoint on the metadata branch.
   994→func (env *TestEnv) ValidateCheckpoint(v CheckpointValidation) {
   995→	env.T.Helper()
   996→
   997→	metadataBranch := "entire/checkpoints/v1"
   998→
   999→	// Compute sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
  1000→	if len(v.CheckpointID) < 3 {
  1001→		env.T.Fatalf("Checkpoint ID too short: %s", v.CheckpointID)
  1002→	}
  1003→	shardedPath := v.CheckpointID[:2] + "/" + v.CheckpointID[2:]
  1004→
  1005→	// Validate root metadata.json exists and has expected fields
  1006→	summaryPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1007→	summaryContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, summaryPath)
  1008→	if !found {
  1009→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary not found at %s", summaryPath)
  1010→	} else {
  1011→		var summary map[string]any
  1012→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary); err != nil {
  1013→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse CheckpointSummary: %v", err)
  1014→		} else {
  1015→			// Validate checkpoint_id
  1016→			if cpID, ok := summary["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1017→				env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", summary["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1018→			}
  1019→			// Validate strategy if specified
  1020→			if v.Strategy != "" {
  1021→				if strategy, ok := summary["strategy"].(string); !ok || strategy != v.Strategy {
  1022→					env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.strategy = %v, want %s", summary["strategy"], v.Strategy)
  1023→				}
  1024→			}
  1025→			// Validate sessions array exists
  1026→			if sessions, ok := summary["sessions"].([]any); !ok || len(sessions) == 0 {
  1027→				env.T.Error("CheckpointSummary.sessions should have at least one entry")
  1028→			}
  1029→			// Validate files_touched if specified
  1030→			if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1031→				if filesTouched, ok := summary["files_touched"].([]any); ok {
  1032→					touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1033→					for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1034→						if s, ok := f.(string); ok {
  1035→							touchedSet[s] = true
  1036→						}
  1037→					}
  1038→					for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1039→						if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1040→							env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.files_touched missing %q", expected)
  1041→						}
  1042→					}
  1043→				}
  1044→			}
  1045→		}
  1046→	}
  1047→
  1048→	// Validate session metadata.json exists
  1049→	sessionMetadataPath := shardedPath + "/0/metadata.json"
  1050→	sessionContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, sessionMetadataPath)
  1051→	if !found {
  1052→		env.T.Errorf("Session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1053→	} else {
  1054→		var metadata map[string]any
  1055→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &metadata); err != nil {
  1056→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse session metadata: %v", err)
  1057→		} else {
  1058→			// Validate checkpoint_id matches
  1059→			if cpID, ok := metadata["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1060→				env.T.Errorf("Session metadata.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", metadata["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1061→			}
  1062→			// Validate created_at exists
  1063→			if _, ok := metadata["created_at"].(string); !ok {
  1064→				env.T.Error("Session metadata.created_at should exist")
  1065→			}
  1066→		}
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL (only enforce for Claude Code, log warnings for others)
  1070→	transcriptPath := shardedPath + "/0/full.jsonl"
  1071→	transcriptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, transcriptPath)
  1072→	if !found {
  1073→		env.T.Errorf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1074→	} else {
  1075→		// Check each line is valid JSON
  1076→		// Only enforce JSONL format for Claude Code - other agents may have different formats
  1077→		isClaudeCode := env.Agent.Name() == AgentNameClaudeCode
  1078→		lines := strings.Split(transcriptContent, "\n")
  1079→		validLines := 0
  1080→		for i, line := range lines {
  1081→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1082→			if line == "" {
  1083→				continue
  1084→			}
  1085→			validLines++
  1086→			var obj map[string]any
  1087→			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
  1088→				if isClaudeCode {
  1089→					env.T.Errorf("Transcript line %d is not valid JSON: %v", i+1, err)
  1090→				}
  1091→				// For non-Claude agents, just log (transcript format may differ)
  1092→			}
  1093→		}
  1094→		if validLines == 0 {
  1095→			env.T.Error("Transcript is empty (no valid JSONL lines)")
  1096→		}
  1097→		// Check expected content
  1098→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedTranscriptContent {
  1099→			if !strings.Contains(transcriptContent, expected) {
  1100→				env.T.Errorf("Transcript should contain %q", expected)
  1101→			}
  1102→		}
  1103→	}
  1104→
  1105→	// Validate prompt.txt contains expected prompts
  1106→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
  1107→		promptPath := shardedPath + "/0/prompt.txt"
  1108→		promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, promptPath)
  1109→		if !found {
  1110→			env.T.Errorf("Prompt file not found at %s", promptPath)
  1111→		} else {
  1112→			for _, expected := range v.ExpectedPrompts {
  1113→				if !strings.Contains(promptContent, expected) {
  1114→					env.T.Errorf("Prompt file should contain %q", expected)
  1115→				}
  1116→			}
  1117→		}
  1118→	}
  1119→
  1120→	// Validate content hash exists and matches transcript
  1121→	hashPath := shardedPath + "/0/content_hash.txt"
  1122→	hashContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, hashPath)
  1123→	if !found {
  1124→		env.T.Errorf("Content hash not found at %s", hashPath)
  1125→	} else if transcriptContent != "" {
  1126→		// Verify hash matches
  1127→		hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcriptContent))
  1128→		expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
  1129→		storedHash := strings.TrimSpace(hashContent)
  1130→		if storedHash != expectedHash {
  1131→			env.T.Errorf("Content hash mismatch:\n  stored:   %s\n  expected: %s", storedHash, expectedHash)
  1132→		}
  1133→	}
  1134→}
  1135→
```

     4→
     5→The Entire CLI currently integrates with Claude Code (stable) and Gemini CLI (preview) via a generic Agent interface. GitHub Copilot CLI is a standalone terminal agent (`copilot` command) that supports hooks similar to Claude/Gemini. This plan adds Copilot as a third agent integration (preview status).
     6→
     7→**Good news**: All Copilot hooks include common fields `sessionId`, `transcript_path`, and `hookEventName` in their stdin JSON. Additionally, `agentStop` provides TurnEnd support. This means the integration follows the same pattern as Claude/Gemini.
     8→
     9→---
    10→
    11→## Hook Inventory (8 hooks)
    12→
    13→| Copilot Hook | CLI Subcommand | Normalized Event | Notes |
    14→|---|---|---|---|
    15→| `sessionStart` | `session-start` | `SessionStart` | Session begins or resumes |
    16→| `sessionEnd` | `session-end` | `SessionEnd` | Session completes/terminated |
    17→| `userPromptSubmitted` | `user-prompt-submitted` | `TurnStart` | User submits prompt |
    18→| `agentStop` | `agent-stop` | `TurnEnd` | Agent finished responding |
    19→| `subagentStop` | `subagent-stop` | `SubagentEnd` | Subagent completed |
    20→| `preToolUse` | `pre-tool-use` | `nil` | Pass-through |
    21→| `postToolUse` | `post-tool-use` | `nil` | Pass-through |
    22→| `errorOccurred` | `error-occurred` | `nil` | Logging only |
    23→
    24→**Common stdin fields** (all hooks): `timestamp`, `cwd`, `sessionId`, `hookEventName`, `transcript_path`
    25→
    26→**Hook-specific fields**: `source`/`initialPrompt` (sessionStart), `reason` (sessionEnd), `prompt` (userPromptSubmitted), `toolName`/`toolArgs` (preToolUse/postToolUse), `toolResult` (postToolUse), `error` (errorOccurred)
    27→
    28→---
    29→
    30→## Step 0: Verify Hook stdin Format
    31→
    32→**Before writing code**, capture actual stdin JSON to confirm the common fields and `agentStop`/`subagentStop` format:
    33→
    34→```json
    35→{
    36→  "version": 1,
    37→  "hooks": {
    38→    "sessionStart": [{"type": "command", "bash": "cat > /tmp/copilot-session-start.json", "timeoutSec": 10}],
    39→    "agentStop": [{"type": "command", "bash": "cat > /tmp/copilot-agent-stop.json", "timeoutSec": 10}],
    40→    "userPromptSubmitted": [{"type": "command", "bash": "cat > /tmp/copilot-user-prompt.json", "timeoutSec": 10}]
    41→  }
    42→}
    43→```
    44→
    45→Place at `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`, run `copilot`, check `/tmp/copilot-*.json`. Confirm `sessionId` and `transcript_path` are present.
    46→
    47→---
    48→
    49→## Step 1: Add Registry Constants
    50→
    51→**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go`
    52→
    53→```go
    54→// In AgentName constants:
    55→AgentNameCopilot AgentName = "github-copilot"
    56→
    57→// In AgentType constants:
    58→AgentTypeCopilot AgentType = "GitHub Copilot"
    59→```
    60→
    61→---
    62→
    63→## Step 2: Create `cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/types.go`
    64→
    65→Follow `geminicli/types.go` pattern. Copilot uses camelCase JSON fields.
    66→
    67→**Hook config types:**
    68→```go
    69→type CopilotHookConfig struct {
    70→    Version int                           `json:"version"`
    71→    Hooks   map[string][]CopilotHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    72→}
    73→
    74→type CopilotHookEntry struct {
    75→    Type       string `json:"type"`
    76→    Bash       string `json:"bash"`
    77→    TimeoutSec int    `json:"timeoutSec,omitempty"`
    78→}
    79→```
    80→
    81→**Common stdin base (embedded in all hook input structs):**
    82→```go
    83→type hookBase struct {
    84→    SessionID      string `json:"sessionId"`
    85→    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    86→    Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    87→    HookEventName  string `json:"hookEventName"`
    88→    Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    89→}
    90→```
    91→
    92→**Hook-specific stdin types:**
    93→- `sessionStartRaw` - embeds `hookBase` + `Source string`, `InitialPrompt string`
    94→- `sessionEndRaw` - embeds `hookBase` + `Reason string`
    95→- `userPromptRaw` - embeds `hookBase` + `Prompt string`
    96→- `agentStopRaw` - embeds `hookBase` (no extra fields expected)
    97→- `subagentStopRaw` - embeds `hookBase` (+ possibly subagent metadata)
    98→- `toolUseRaw` - embeds `hookBase` + `ToolName string`, `ToolArgs json.RawMessage`, `ToolResult json.RawMessage`
    99→- `errorRaw` - embeds `hookBase` + `Error json.RawMessage`
   100→
   101→**Tool name constants:**
   102→```go
   103→const (
   104→    ToolEdit   = "edit"
   105→    ToolCreate = "create"
   106→)
   107→var FileModificationTools = []string{ToolEdit, ToolCreate}
   108→```
   109→
   110→---
   111→
   112→## Step 3: Create `cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/copilot.go`
   113→
   114→Core agent. Follow `geminicli/gemini.go` as template.
   115→
   116→**Self-registration:**
   117→```go
   118→func init() {
   119→    agent.Register(agent.AgentNameCopilot, NewCopilotAgent)
   120→}
   121→```
   122→
   123→**Identity:**
   124→- `Name()` → `agent.AgentNameCopilot`
   125→- `Type()` → `agent.AgentTypeCopilot`
   126→- `Description()` → `"GitHub Copilot - GitHub's AI coding assistant"`
   127→- `IsPreview()` → `true`
   128→- `ProtectedDirs()` → `[]string{}` (`.github` is shared, not ours)
   129→
   130→**Detection** (`DetectPresence()`):
   131→- Check for `.github/hooks/` with an Entire copilot hook file present (AreHooksInstalled)
   132→- OR check `~/.copilot/` directory existence
   133→- Use `paths.RepoRoot()` for repo-relative checks
   134→
   135→**Session storage:**
   136→- `GetHookConfigPath()` → `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`
   137→- `GetSessionDir(repoPath)` → `~/.copilot/session-state/` (with `ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR` override)
   138→- `ResolveSessionFile(dir, sessionID)` → `filepath.Join(dir, sessionID, "events.jsonl")`
   139→- `FormatResumeCommand(sessionID)` → `copilot --resume`
   140→
   141→**Transcript storage** (JSONL - reuse existing helpers):
   142→- `ReadTranscript(sessionRef)` → `os.ReadFile(sessionRef)`
   143→- `ChunkTranscript(content, maxSize)` → `agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)`
   144→- `ReassembleTranscript(chunks)` → `agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks)`
   145→
   146→**Legacy methods:** ParseHookInput, GetSessionID, ReadSession, WriteSession (follow Gemini pattern)
   147→
   148→---
   149→
   150→## Step 4: Create `cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/lifecycle.go`
   151→
   152→Event parsing. Follow `geminicli/lifecycle.go` pattern.
   153→
   154→**Compile-time assertions:**
   155→```go
   156→var _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
   157→```
   158→
   159→**`ParseHookEvent` switch:**
   160→```go
   161→case HookNameSessionStart  → parseSessionStart(stdin)  → agent.SessionStart
   162→case HookNameUserPromptSubmitted → parseTurnStart(stdin) → agent.TurnStart (with Prompt)
   163→case HookNameAgentStop     → parseTurnEnd(stdin)        → agent.TurnEnd
   164→case HookNameSessionEnd    → parseSessionEnd(stdin)     → agent.SessionEnd
   165→case HookNameSubagentStop  → parseSubagentEnd(stdin)    → agent.SubagentEnd
   166→case HookNamePreToolUse, HookNamePostToolUse, HookNameErrorOccurred → nil
   167→```
   168→
   169→**Internal parse functions** (use `agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[T]` generic helper):
   170→- `parseSessionStart` → reads `sessionStartRaw`, returns Event{SessionStart, SessionID, SessionRef}
   171→- `parseTurnStart` → reads `userPromptRaw`, returns Event{TurnStart, SessionID, SessionRef, Prompt}
   172→- `parseTurnEnd` → reads `agentStopRaw`, returns Event{TurnEnd, SessionID, SessionRef}
   173→- `parseSessionEnd` → reads `sessionEndRaw`, returns Event{SessionEnd, SessionID, SessionRef}
   174→- `parseSubagentEnd` → reads `subagentStopRaw`, returns Event{SubagentEnd, SessionID, SessionRef}
   175→
   176→**TranscriptAnalyzer methods:**
   177→- `GetTranscriptPosition(path)` → count lines in JSONL file
   178→- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path, offset)` → parse JSONL from line offset, extract file paths from edit/create events
   179→- `ExtractPrompts(sessionRef, fromOffset)` → extract user message content from JSONL
   180→- `ExtractSummary(sessionRef)` → last assistant message from JSONL
   181→
   182→---
   183→
   184→## Step 5: Create `cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/hooks.go`
   185→
   186→Hook installation into `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`. Simpler than Claude/Gemini since config is a flat JSON file (no matchers).
   187→
   188→**Constants:**
   189→```go
   190→const (
   191→    HookNameSessionStart       = "session-start"
   192→    HookNameSessionEnd         = "session-end"
   193→    HookNameUserPromptSubmitted = "user-prompt-submitted"
   194→    HookNameAgentStop          = "agent-stop"
   195→    HookNameSubagentStop       = "subagent-stop"
   196→    HookNamePreToolUse         = "pre-tool-use"
   197→    HookNamePostToolUse        = "post-tool-use"
   198→    HookNameErrorOccurred      = "error-occurred"
   199→)
   200→
   201→// Maps CLI subcommand names to Copilot's native hook names (camelCase in config)
   202→var hookNameToConfigKey = map[string]string{
   203→    HookNameSessionStart:        "sessionStart",
   204→    HookNameSessionEnd:          "sessionEnd",
   205→    HookNameUserPromptSubmitted: "userPromptSubmitted",
   206→    HookNameAgentStop:           "agentStop",
   207→    HookNameSubagentStop:        "subagentStop",
   208→    HookNamePreToolUse:          "preToolUse",
   209→    HookNamePostToolUse:         "postToolUse",
   210→    HookNameErrorOccurred:       "errorOccurred",
   211→}
   212→```
   213→
   214→**`InstallHooks(localDev, force)`:**
   215→1. `paths.RepoRoot()` to find repo root
   216→2. Read/create `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`
   217→3. Parse into `CopilotHookConfig` (or create with `version: 1`)
   218→4. For idempotency: check if existing commands match expected
   219→5. Remove existing Entire hooks if force or mode change
   220→6. For each of 8 hooks, add entry:
   221→   ```json
   222→   {"type": "command", "bash": "entire hooks github-copilot <subcommand>", "timeoutSec": 10}
   223→   ```
   224→7. Create `.github/hooks/` dir if needed
   225→8. Write back with `json.MarshalIndent`
   226→9. Return count (8)
   227→
   228→**`UninstallHooks()`:** Remove entries matching `entireHookPrefixes`, remove config key if array empty
   229→
   230→**`AreHooksInstalled()`:** Check if any entry matches `entireHookPrefixes`
   231→
   232→**`entireHookPrefixes`:** `["entire ", "go run ${COPILOT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go "]`
   233→
   234→---
   235→
   236→## Step 6: Create `cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot/transcript.go`
   237→
   238→Transcript parsing for `events.jsonl`. Basic implementation, refined after verifying actual format in Step 0.
   239→
   240→Since Copilot uses JSONL (like Claude Code), the transcript parsing follows the Claude pattern - line-based offsets, JSONL line splitting, etc. The exact event schema will be adapted based on the actual `events.jsonl` content discovered in Step 0.
   241→
   242→**Key functions:**
   243→- Parse JSONL events line by line
   244→- Extract file paths from tool call events (edit, create tools)
   245→- Extract user prompts from user message events
   246→- Extract assistant responses for summaries
   247→- Token usage calculation (if token fields are present in events)
   248→
   249→---
